using Microsoft.Maui;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls;

public class ColorPicker : ContentView, IDrawable
{
  public event EventHandler<Color>? ColorChanged;
  public float Hue { get; set; }
  public float Saturation { get; set; }
  public float Luminosity { get; set; }

  private GraphicsView graphicsView;
  private short displayMode = 0;

  public ColorPicker()
  {
    Loaded += ColorPicker_Loaded;
    Unloaded += ColorPicker_Unloaded;

    graphicsView = new GraphicsView();
    graphicsView.Drawable = this;
    PointerGestureRecognizer pointerGesture = new();
    pointerGesture.PointerPressed += PointerGesture_PointerPressed;
    graphicsView.GestureRecognizers.Add(pointerGesture);

    Content = graphicsView;
  }

  private void PointerGesture_PointerPressed(object? sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    Point pos = e.GetPosition(this).Value;
    float x = (float)pos.X;
    float y = (float)pos.Y;
    float width = (float)graphicsView.Width;
    float height = (float)graphicsView.Height;
    if (x > 0 && x < width && y > 0 && y < height)
    {
      Hue = x / (width * .9f);
      Saturation = y / (height * .6f);
      Saturation = 1 - Saturation;
      ColorChanged?.Invoke(this, Color.FromHsv(Hue, Saturation, Luminosity));
    }
  }

  private void ColorPicker_Unloaded(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    isRunning = false;
  }
  private void ColorPicker_Loaded(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    isRunning = true;
    Dispatcher.StartTimer(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1), runLoop);
  }

  bool isRunning;
  private bool runLoop()
  {
    graphicsView.Invalidate();
    return isRunning;
  }

  public void Draw(ICanvas canvas, RectF dirtyRect)
  {
    float x = 10, y = 10, botderOffset = 4;
    switch (displayMode)
    {
      case 0:
        canvas.Scale(.6f, .6f);
        float xCord = 0, yCord = 0;
        float counter = .01f;
        float width = (dirtyRect.Width - x) * .9f;
        float[] widths = [width, (dirtyRect.Width - x) - width];
        float height = counter * (dirtyRect.Height - y) * 2;
        width = counter * widths[0] * 2;
        float k = 0;
        for (float j = 1; j >= 0; j -= counter)
        {
          for (float i = 0; i < 1; i += counter)
          {
            canvas.FillColor = Color.FromHsv(1 / 3f, i, j);
            canvas.FillRectangle(i * widths[0], k * height, width, height);
          }
          k += counter;
        }
        for (float i = 1; i >= 0; i -= counter)
        {
          canvas.FillColor = Color.FromHsv(i, 100, 100);
          canvas.FillRectangle(widths[0] + width, i * height, widths[1], height);
        }
        canvas.ResetState();
        break;

      case 1:
        canvas.SaveState();
        canvas.Scale(.9f, .6f);
        xCord = 0;
        yCord = 0;
        counter = .01f;
        width = counter * (dirtyRect.Width - (x + botderOffset)/2) * 2;
        height = counter * (dirtyRect.Height - (y + botderOffset)/2) * 2;
        k = 0;
        for (float j = 1; j >= 0; j -= counter)
        {
          for (float i = 0; i < 1; i += counter)
          {
            canvas.FillColor = Color.FromHsv(i, j, 1);
            canvas.FillRectangle(i * (dirtyRect.Width - (x + botderOffset)) + x / 2, k * (dirtyRect.Height - (y + botderOffset)) + y / 2, width, height);
          }
          k += counter;
        }

        canvas.StrokeSize = botderOffset;
        canvas.DrawRectangle(0, 0, dirtyRect.Width, dirtyRect.Height);
        canvas.RestoreState();
        break;
    }
  }
}

  }

  public GroupBox()
  {
    InitializeComponent();

  }

  private void Title_LayoutUpdated(object sender, object e)
  {
    border.Margin = new Thickness(title.Width + 10, 10, 3, 3);
  }
}

using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System.Reflection;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls;

public partial class PickerColor : ContentView, IDrawable
{
  public event EventHandler<Color>? ColorSelected;
  public List<NamedColors> NamedColors { get; set; }
  public string ColorName { get; set; }
  public Color BorderColor { get; set; }
  public float BorderWidth { get; set; }
  public float Resolution { get; set; }

  public float Hue { get; set; }
  public float Saturation { get; set; }
  public float Luminosity { get; set; }

  private bool displayFirst = true;
  public bool DisplayFirst
  {
    get
    {
      return displayFirst;
    }
    set
    {
      displayFirst = value;
      Init_Bounds();
      _gfvMain.Invalidate();
    }
  }

  private Color newColor, oldColor;
  private RectF[] bounds;
  private byte activeBounds;
  private Grid deadArea;
  private PointF colorPoint;

  public PickerColor()
  {
    newColor = Colors.Red;
    oldColor = Colors.White;
    Resolution = .1f;
    colorPoint = new();
    NamedColors = new();

    InitializeComponent();

    _gfvMain.Drawable = this;

    this.Loaded += PickerColor_Loaded;
    this.Unloaded += PickerColor_Unloaded;
    this.PropertyChanged += PickerColor_PropertyChanged;
  }

  private void PickerColor_PropertyChanged(object? sender, System.ComponentModel.PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
  {
    //_gfvMain.Invalidate();
  }
  private void PickerColor_Loaded(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    Init_Bounds();
    InitColorsNames();

    findRootParent();

    //isRunning = true;
    //Dispatcher.StartTimer(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1), runLoop);
  }
  private void PickerColor_Unloaded(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    isRunning = false;
  }

  private void InitColorsNames()
  {
    // Loop through the public static fields of the Color structure.
    foreach (FieldInfo fieldInfo in typeof(Colors).GetRuntimeFields())
    {
      if (fieldInfo.IsPublic &&
          fieldInfo.IsStatic &&
          fieldInfo.FieldType == typeof(Color))
      {
        // Convert the name to a friendly name.
        string name = fieldInfo.Name;

        // Instantiate a NamedColor object.
        NamedColors namedColor = new NamedColors
        {
          Name = name,
          Color = ((Color)fieldInfo.GetValue(null))
        };

        // Add it to the collection.
        NamedColors.Add(namedColor);
      }
      NamedColors.TrimExcess();
    }

    //BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_lbTooltipColor, NamedColors);
  }

  bool isRunning;
  private bool runLoop()
  {
    //_gfvMain.Invalidate();
    return isRunning;
  }

  private void Init_Bounds()
  {
    bounds = new RectF[3];
    bounds[0] = new RectF(0, 0, (float)(_gfvMain.Width * .8), (float)_gfvMain.Height);
    bounds[1] = new RectF(bounds[0].Width, 0, (float)(_gfvMain.Width - bounds[0].Width) / 2, (float)_gfvMain.Height);
    bounds[2] = new RectF(bounds[0].Width + bounds[1].Width, 0, bounds[1].Width, (float)_gfvMain.Height);
  }
  private void _gfvMain_SizeChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    if (Width > 0 && Height > 0)
    {
      Init_Bounds();
      _gfvMain.Invalidate();
    }
  }

  public void Draw(ICanvas canvas, RectF dirtyRect)
  {
    float x = 0, y = 0;
    float height = Resolution * (bounds[0].Height) * 2;
    float width = Resolution * bounds[0].Width * 2;
    float k = 0, sat = 0;

    switch (DisplayFirst)
    {
      case true:
        if (activeBounds == 2)
          Hue = colorPoint.Y / bounds[0].Height;

        if (activeBounds == 0)
        {
          Saturation = colorPoint.X / bounds[0].Width;
          Saturation = Saturation;
          Luminosity = colorPoint.Y / bounds[0].Height;
          Luminosity = 1 - Luminosity;
        }

        newColor = Color.FromHsv(Hue, Saturation, Luminosity);
        for (float j = 1; j >= 0; j -= Resolution)
        {
          for (float i = 0; i < 1; i += Resolution)
          {
            canvas.FillColor = Color.FromHsv(Hue, i, j);
            canvas.FillRectangle(i * bounds[0].Width, k * bounds[0].Height, width, height);
          }
          k += Resolution;
        }

        // Draw circle
        canvas.StrokeColor = Luminosity < .45f ? Colors.White : Colors.Black;
        canvas.StrokeSize = 2;
        canvas.DrawCircle(colorPoint.X, colorPoint.Y, 10);

        for (float i = 1; i >= 0; i -= Resolution)
        {
          canvas.FillColor = Color.FromHsv(i, 100, 100);
          canvas.FillRectangle(bounds[2].X, i * bounds[2].Height - height, bounds[2].Width, height);
        }
        break;

      case false:
        for (float j = 1; j >= 0; j -= Resolution)
        {
          for (float i = 0; i < 1; i += Resolution)
          {
            canvas.FillColor = Color.FromHsv(i, j, Luminosity);
            canvas.FillRectangle(i * bounds[0].Width, k * bounds[0].Height, width, height);
          }
          k += Resolution;
        }

        // Draw third color bar
        if (activeBounds == 0)
          Hue = colorPoint.X / bounds[0].Width;

        Saturation = colorPoint.Y / bounds[0].Height;
        Saturation = 1 - Saturation;

        if (activeBounds == 2)
        {
          Luminosity = colorPoint.Y / bounds[0].Height;
          Luminosity = 1 - Luminosity;
          sat = newColor.GetSaturation();
        }
        else if (activeBounds == 0)
        {
          sat = Saturation;
        }

        // Draw circle
        canvas.StrokeColor = Luminosity < .45f ? Colors.White : Colors.Black;
        canvas.StrokeSize = 2;
        canvas.DrawCircle(colorPoint.X, colorPoint.Y, 10);

        k = 0;
        for (float i = 1 + Resolution; i >= 0; i -= Resolution)
        {
          canvas.FillColor = Color.FromHsv(Hue, sat, k);
          canvas.FillRectangle(bounds[2].X, i * bounds[2].Height - height, bounds[2].Width, height);
          k += Resolution;
        }

        newColor = Color.FromHsv(Hue, sat, Luminosity);
        break;
    }
    // Draw old and new Color
    k = dirtyRect.Height / 2;
    canvas.FillColor = newColor;
    canvas.FillRectangle(bounds[1].X, bounds[1].Y, bounds[1].Width, k);
    canvas.FillColor = oldColor;
    canvas.FillRectangle(bounds[1].X, k, bounds[1].Width, k);

    //canvas.StrokeSize = BorderWidth;
    //canvas.StrokeColor = BorderColor;
    //canvas.DrawRectangle(0, 0, dirtyRect.Width, dirtyRect.Height);
  }

  bool isPointerPressed; PointF mousePoint;
  public void PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerMoved(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    if (isPointerPressed)
    {
      mousePoint = e.GetPosition(this).Value;

      colorPoint.X = (float)Math.Clamp(mousePoint.X, bounds[activeBounds].X, bounds[activeBounds].X + bounds[activeBounds].Width);
      colorPoint.Y = (float)Math.Clamp(mousePoint.Y, bounds[activeBounds].Y, bounds[activeBounds].Y + bounds[activeBounds].Height);

      _elpColor.TranslationX = colorPoint.X - _elpColor.Width / 2;
      _elpColor.TranslationY = colorPoint.Y - _elpColor.Height / 2;
      _elpColor.Stroke = Luminosity < .45f ? Colors.White : Colors.Black;
      _elpColor.Fill = newColor;
    }

    setColor();
  }

  private void PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerPressed(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    isPointerPressed = true;
    _lbTooltipColor.IsVisible = _elpColor.IsVisible = true;
    deadArea.IsVisible = true;

    oldColor = newColor;

    mousePoint = e.GetPosition(this).Value;

    if (mousePoint.X < bounds[0].Width)
    {
      activeBounds = 0;
    }
    else if (mousePoint.X >= bounds[1].X && mousePoint.X < bounds[1].X + bounds[1].Width)
    {
      activeBounds = 1;
    }
    else
    {
      activeBounds = 2;
    }

    PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerMoved(sender, e);
  }
  public void PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerExited(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    isPointerPressed = false;
    _lbTooltipColor.IsVisible = _elpColor.IsVisible = false;
    deadArea.IsVisible = false;
  }
  public void PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerReleased(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    isPointerPressed = false;
    _lbTooltipColor.IsVisible = _elpColor.IsVisible = false;
    deadArea.IsVisible = false;
  }

  private void findRootParent()
  {
    var parent = Parent;
    while (parent != null)
    {
      if (parent is Page)
      {
        break;
      }
      parent = parent.Parent;
    }
    if (parent is ContentPage page)
    {
      Grid rootGrid = (Grid)page.Content;
      deadArea = new Grid();
      rootGrid.Children.Add(deadArea);
      Grid.SetColumn(deadArea, 0);
      Grid.SetColumnSpan(deadArea, rootGrid.ColumnDefinitions.Count);
      Grid.SetRow(deadArea, 0);
      Grid.SetRowSpan(deadArea, rootGrid.RowDefinitions.Count);
      deadArea.GestureRecognizers.Add(new PointerGestureRecognizer());
      ((PointerGestureRecognizer)deadArea.GestureRecognizers[0]).PointerMoved += PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerMoved;
      ((PointerGestureRecognizer)deadArea.GestureRecognizers[0]).PointerReleased += PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerReleased;
      ((PointerGestureRecognizer)deadArea.GestureRecognizers[0]).PointerExited += PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerExited;

      deadArea.BackgroundColor = Colors.Transparent;
      deadArea.IsVisible = false;
    }
  }

  bool isRGB = true;
  private void SwitchColorMode_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    isRGB = !isRGB;
    if (isRGB) ((Button)sender).Text = "RGB";
    else ((Button)sender).Text = "HSV";
  }

  private void setColor()
  {
    displayColorText();

    _gfvMain.Invalidate();

    string colorValue = newColor.ToHex();
    for (int i = 0; i < NamedColors.Count; i++)
    {
      if (colorValue == NamedColors[i].Name)
      {
        ColorName = NamedColors[i].Name;
      }
    }
    _lbTooltipColor.TranslationX = _elpColor.TranslationX;
    _lbTooltipColor.TranslationY = _elpColor.TranslationY - 100;
    _lbTooltipColor.Text = ColorName;

    ColorSelected?.Invoke(this, newColor);
  }

  private void displayColorText()
  {
    int r, g, b;

    if (isRGB)
    {
      r = (byte)(newColor.Red * 255);
      g = (byte)(newColor.Green * 255);
      b = (byte)(newColor.Blue * 255);
    }
    else
    {
      r = (int)(Hue * 360);
      g = (int)(Saturation * 100);
      b = (int)(Luminosity * 100);
    }

    _entry1.Text = r.ToString();
    _entry2.Text = g.ToString();
    _entry3.Text = b.ToString();
  }
}

    <!-- World Map -->

    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="13" Text="Update" Margin="5,1" Clicked="Update_Clicked"/>

    <!-- Templates -->
    <Border Grid.Column="0" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="15" Margin="10,10,-4,0">
      <ScrollView>
        <FlexLayout x:Name="_flxTemplates" Wrap="Wrap" AlignItems="Start" AlignContent="Start" JustifyContent="Start">
          <BindableLayout.ItemTemplate>
            <DataTemplate>
              <Border WidthRequest="40" HeightRequest="40" BackgroundColor="{Binding BackgroundColor}" Stroke="{Binding Stroke}" StrokeThickness="{OnPlatform Android=2, WinUI=2}">
                <Label Text="{Binding Text}" TextColor="{Binding TextColor}" FontSize="{OnPlatform Android=8, WinUI=8}" HorizontalTextAlignment="Center" VerticalTextAlignment="Center"/>
                <Border.GestureRecognizers>
                  <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="TapGR_Tapped"/>
                  <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="DoubleTapGR_MapPart" NumberOfTapsRequired="2"/>
                </Border.GestureRecognizers>
              </Border>
            </DataTemplate>
          </BindableLayout.ItemTemplate>
        </FlexLayout>
      </ScrollView>
    </Border>
    <Label Grid.Column="0" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="15" x:Name="_lbTemplates" Text="No Templates" TextColor="Gray" HorizontalOptions="Center" VerticalOptions="Center" Margin="30,20,10,10"/>
    <!-- Edit Templates 34 -->
    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="15" x:Name="_btnAddTemplate" Text="Add" VerticalOptions="Start" Margin="14,-8,14,0" Clicked="BtnAddTemplate_Clicked" IsEnabled="False"/>
    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="15" x:Name="_btnSaveTemplate" Text="Save" VerticalOptions="Start" Margin="14,27,14,0" Clicked="BtnSaveTemplates_Clicked" IsEnabled="False"/>
    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="15" x:Name="_btnLoadTemplate" Text="Load" VerticalOptions="Start" Margin="14,63,14,0" Clicked="BtnLoadTemplates_Clicked"/>
    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="16" x:Name="_btnClearTemplate" Text="Clear" VerticalOptions="Start" Margin="14,99,14,0" Clicked="BtnClearTemplates_Clicked" IsEnabled="False"/>

    <!-- Save and Load 37 -->
    <Entry Grid.Column="0" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="16" Placeholder="Filename" HorizontalOptions="Fill" Margin="10,0,0,0"/>
    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="16" Text="Save" Padding="4,0" Margin="0,0,45,0" HorizontalOptions="Center" Clicked="BtnSaveClicked"/>
    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="16" Text="Load" Padding="4,0" Margin="45,0,0,0" HorizontalOptions="Center" Clicked="BtnLoadClicked"/>

    <!-- Picker Color 41 -->
    <cc:PickerColor Grid.Column="0" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="8" Grid.RowSpan="6" x:Name="_pickerColor" Margin="0,0,0,0" Scale=".9" ColorSelected="_pickerColor_ColorSelected" IsClippedToBounds="False" ZIndex="100"/>
    <Button Grid.Column="2" Grid.Row="11" Text="Mode 1" Margin="5,-2,5,1" Clicked="ColorsModeChange_Clicked"/>

    <!-- Part Type 42 -->
    <Label Grid.Column="0" Grid.ColumnSpan="1" Grid.Row="17" Grid.RowSpan="1" Text="Type : "/>
    <Picker Grid.Column="0" Grid.ColumnSpan="3" Grid.Row="17" Grid.RowSpan="1" x:Name="_pckPartType" VerticalOptions="Center" Margin="60,0,10,0" SelectedIndexChanged="PckPartType_SelectedIndexChanged"/>
    <!-- HitPoints 46 -->
    <Label Grid.Column="0" Grid.Row="18" Text="Hit Points"/>
    <Entry Grid.Column="1" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="18" x:Name="_etyHitPoints" Margin="50,0,24,0" Text="0" MaxLength="6" TextChanged="EtySkillPoints_TextChanged"/>
    <Label Grid.Column="1" Grid.Row="19" Text="Max"/>
    <Entry Grid.Column="1" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="19" x:Name="_etyHitPointsMax" Margin="50,0,24,0" Text="0" MaxLength="6" TextChanged="EtySkillPoints_TextChanged"/>
    <!-- MagicPoints 50 -->
    <Label Grid.Column="0" Grid.Row="20" Text="Magic Points"/>
    <Entry Grid.Column="1" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="20" x:Name="_etyMagicPoints" Margin="50,0,24,0" Text="0" MaxLength="6" TextChanged="EtySkillPoints_TextChanged"/>
    <Label Grid.Column="1" Grid.Row="21" Text="Max"/>
    <Entry Grid.Column="1" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="21" x:Name="_etyMagicPointsMax" Margin="50,0,24,0" Text="0" MaxLength="6" TextChanged="EtySkillPoints_TextChanged"/>
    <!-- StaminaPoints 54 -->
    <Label Grid.Column="0" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="22" Text="Stamina Points"/>
    <Entry Grid.Column="1" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="22" x:Name="_etyStaminaPoints" Text="0" Margin="50,0,24,0" MaxLength="6" TextChanged="EtySkillPoints_TextChanged"/>
    <Label Grid.Column="1" Grid.Row="23" Text="Max"/>
    <Entry Grid.Column="1" Grid.ColumnSpan="2" Grid.Row="23" x:Name="_etyStaminaPointsMax" Text="0" Margin="50,0,24,0" MaxLength="6" TextChanged="EtySkillPoints_TextChanged"/>
    <!-- EnergyPoints 58 -->

    <!-- Defense 62 -->

using Microsoft.Maui.Animations;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls.Shapes;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics.Text;
using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls;

public delegate void MapPartEventArs(IMapPart mapPart);
public partial class Properies : ScrollView
{
  public ObservableCollection<MapPartModel> Templates { get; set; } = new();
  public ObservableCollection<MapPartType> PartTypes { get; set; } = new();

  public event MapPartEventArs UpdateMapPartEvent;
  public event EventHandler SaveMapPartEvent, LoadMapPartEvent, ClearMapPartEvent;
  public IMapPart MapPart { get; private set; }

  /// <summary>
  /// Stroke = 0, Background = 1, TextColor = 2
  /// </summary>
  private int activeColor;

  public Properies()
  {
    PartTypes.Add(MapPartType.Player);
    PartTypes.Add(MapPartType.Other);
    PartTypes.Add(MapPartType.NPC);
    PartTypes.Add(MapPartType.Item);
    PartTypes.Add(MapPartType.Object);
    PartTypes.Add(MapPartType.Map);

    MapPart = new MapPartModel();

    InitializeComponent();

    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_flxTemplates, Templates);
    _pckPartType.ItemsSource = PartTypes;
    //_pckPartType.SelectedIndex = 5;
  }

  private void ClearMap_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    ClearMapPartEvent?.Invoke(null, null);
  }
  private void Update_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    switch (_pckPartType.SelectedIndex)
    {
      case 0:// Player:
        if (MapPart is not CharacterModel)
          MapPart = new CharacterModel();
        ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats = new StatsModel()
        {
          HitPoints = int.Parse(_etyHitPoints.Text),
          HitPointsMax = int.Parse(_etyHitPointsMax.Text),
          MagicPoints = int.Parse(_etyMagicPoints.Text),
          MagicPointsMax = int.Parse(_etyMagicPointsMax.Text),
          StaminaPoints = int.Parse(_etyStaminaPoints.Text),
          StaminaPointsMax = int.Parse(_etyStaminaPointsMax.Text),
          EnergyPoints = int.Parse(_etyEnergyPoints.Text),
          EnergyPointsMax = int.Parse(_etyEnergyPointsMax.Text),
          Offense = int.Parse(_etyOffense.Text),
          Defense = int.Parse(_etyDefense.Text),
          Resistance = int.Parse(_etyResistance.Text),
          MagicPower = int.Parse(_etyMagicPower.Text),
          Speed = int.Parse(_etySpeed.Text),
          SkillPoints = int.Parse(_etySkillPoints.Text),
        };
        ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Race = new RaceModel() { Name = "Human", Description = "Description of Humans" };
        break;
      case 1:// Other:
        break;
      case 2:// NPC:
        break;
      case 3:// Item:
        break;
      case 4:// Object:
        break;
      case 5:// Map:
        if (MapPart is not MapPartModel)
          MapPart = new MapPartModel();
        break;
    }

    MapPart.PartType = (MapPartType)_pckPartType.SelectedIndex + 1;

    MapPart.Parent = int.Parse(_lbParent.Text);
    MapPart.Child = int.Parse(_lbChild.Text);
    MapPart.Text = _entryText.Text;
    MapPart.Description = _editorDescription.Text;
    MapPart.Stroke = _btnColor.BorderColor;
    MapPart.BackgroundColor = _btnColor.BackgroundColor;
    MapPart.TextColor = _btnColor.TextColor;
    MapPart.IsEnable = _chbEnable.IsChecked;
    MapPart.IsVisible = _chbVisible.IsChecked;
    MapPart.ColumnRow = [byte.Parse(_lbColumn.Text), byte.Parse(_lbRow.Text)];

    UpdateMapPartEvent?.Invoke(MapPart);
  }

  private void _btnStroke_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    activeColor = 0;
    UpdateButtonColor((Button)sender, _entryStroke);
  }
  private void _btnBG_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    activeColor = 1;
    UpdateButtonColor((Button)sender, _entryBG);
  }
  private void _btnTextColor_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    activeColor = 2;
    UpdateButtonColor((Button)sender, _entryTextColor);
  }
  private void UpdateButtonColor(Button btnColor, Entry entryColor)
  {
    switch (activeColor)
    {
      case 0:
        btnColor.BackgroundColor = _btnColor.BorderColor;
        _btnStroke.BorderWidth = 2;
        _btnBG.BorderWidth = 0;
        _btnTextColor.BorderWidth = 0;
        Grid.SetRow(_rrectColorActive, 5);
        break;
      case 1:
        btnColor.BackgroundColor = _btnColor.BackgroundColor;
        _btnStroke.BorderWidth = 0;
        _btnBG.BorderWidth = 2;
        _btnTextColor.BorderWidth = 0;
        Grid.SetRow(_rrectColorActive, 6);
        break;
      case 2:
        btnColor.BackgroundColor = _btnColor.TextColor;
        _btnStroke.BorderWidth = 0;
        _btnBG.BorderWidth = 0;
        _btnTextColor.BorderWidth = 2;
        Grid.SetRow(_rrectColorActive, 7);
        break;
    }

    entryColor.Text = btnColor.BackgroundColor.ToHex().Substring(1);
  }

  private void BtnSaveClicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    SaveMapPartEvent?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
  }
  private void BtnLoadClicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    LoadMapPartEvent?.Invoke(this, EventArgs.Empty);
  }

  private void BtnAddTemplate_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    _lbTemplates.IsVisible = false;
    MapPartModel template = new() { TextColor = _btnColor.TextColor, BackgroundColor = _btnColor.BackgroundColor, Stroke = _btnColor.BorderColor };
    template.Text = (Templates.Count + 1).ToString();
    Templates.Add(template);
    _btnSaveTemplate.IsEnabled = true;
    _btnClearTemplate.IsEnabled = true;
  }
  private void BtnSaveTemplates_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    SaveMapPartEvent?.Invoke(this, null);
  }
  private void BtnLoadTemplates_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    LoadMapPartEvent?.Invoke(this, null);
  }
  private void BtnClearTemplates_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    Templates.Clear();
    _btnAddTemplate.IsEnabled = true;
    _btnLoadTemplate.IsEnabled = true;
    _btnSaveTemplate.IsEnabled = false;
    _btnClearTemplate.IsEnabled = false;
    _lbTemplates.IsVisible = true;
  }

  private void _pickerColor_ColorSelected(object sender, Color e)
  {
    switch (activeColor)
    {
      case 0:
        _btnColor.BorderColor = e;
        break;
      case 1:
        _btnColor.BackgroundColor = e;
        break;
      case 2:
        _btnColor.TextColor = e;
        break;
    }
  }

  private void TapGR_Tapped(object sender, TappedEventArgs e)
  {
    int index = _flxTemplates.Children.IndexOf((IView)sender);
    _btnStroke.Background = Templates[index].Stroke;
    _btnColor.BorderColor = ((SolidColorBrush)_btnStroke.Background).Color;
    _btnColor.BackgroundColor = _btnBG.BackgroundColor = Templates[index].BackgroundColor;
    _btnColor.TextColor = _btnTextColor.BackgroundColor = Templates[index].TextColor;
  }

  private void DoubleTapGR_MapPart(object sender, TappedEventArgs e)
  {
    int index = _flxTemplates.Children.IndexOf((IView)sender);
    Templates.RemoveAt(index);
    Border border; Label label;
    for (int i = index; i < Templates.Count; i++)
    {
      Templates[i].Text = (i + 1).ToString();
      border = (Border)_flxTemplates.Children[i];
      label = (Label)border.Content;
      label.Text = Templates[i].Text;
    }
  }
  private void ColorsModeChange_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    _pickerColor.DisplayFirst = !_pickerColor.DisplayFirst;
    if (_pickerColor.DisplayFirst) ((Button)sender).Text = "Mode 1";
    else ((Button)sender).Text = "Mode 2";
  }

  public void SetMapPart(IMapPart mapPart)
  {
    MapPart = mapPart;
    _lbParent.Text = MapPart.Parent.ToString(); // Prent
    _lbChild.Text = MapPart.Child.ToString(); // Child
    _entryText.Text = MapPart.Text; // Text
    _editorDescription.Text = MapPart.Description; // Description
    _lbColumn.Text = MapPart.ColumnRow[0].ToString(); // Column
    _lbRow.Text = MapPart.ColumnRow[1].ToString(); // Row

    // Border
    _btnStroke.BackgroundColor = ((SolidColorBrush)MapPart.Stroke).Color;
    // BackgroundColor
    _btnBG.BackgroundColor = MapPart.BackgroundColor;
    // TextColor
    _btnTextColor.BackgroundColor = MapPart.TextColor;

    // Stroke
    _btnColor.BorderColor = _btnStroke.BackgroundColor;
    // BackgroundColor
    _btnColor.BackgroundColor = _btnBG.BackgroundColor;
    // TextColor
    _btnColor.TextColor = _btnTextColor.BackgroundColor;

    _chbEnable.IsChecked = MapPart.IsEnable; // IsEnable
    _chbVisible.IsChecked = MapPart.IsVisible; // IsVisible

    _btnAddTemplate.IsEnabled = true; // _btnAdd
    //_btnSaveTemplate.IsEnabled = false; // _btnSave
    //_btnLoadTemplate.IsEnabled = true; // _btnLoad
    //_btnClearTemplate.IsEnabled = false; // _btnClear

    _pckPartType.SelectedIndex = (byte)MapPart.PartType - 1;
    switch (MapPart.PartType)
    {
      case MapPartType.Player:
        _etyHitPoints.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.HitPoints.ToString();
        _etyHitPointsMax.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.HitPointsMax.ToString();

        _etyMagicPoints.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.MagicPoints.ToString();
        _etyMagicPointsMax.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.MagicPointsMax.ToString();

        _etyStaminaPoints.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.StaminaPoints.ToString();
        _etyStaminaPointsMax.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.StaminaPointsMax.ToString();

        _etyEnergyPoints.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.EnergyPoints.ToString();
        _etyEnergyPointsMax.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.EnergyPointsMax.ToString();

        _etyDefense.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.Defense.ToString();
        _etyOffense.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.Offense.ToString();
        _etyResistance.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.Resistance.ToString();
        _etyMagicPower.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.MagicPower.ToString();
        _etySpeed.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.Speed.ToString();
        _etySkillPoints.Text = ((CharacterModel)MapPart).Stats.SkillPoints.ToString();
        break;
      case MapPartType.Other:
        break;
      case MapPartType.NPC:
        break;
      case MapPartType.Item:
        break;
      case MapPartType.Object:
        break;
      case MapPartType.Map:
      default:
        _etyHitPoints.Text = "";
        _etyHitPointsMax.Text = "";
        _etyMagicPoints.Text = "";
        _etyMagicPointsMax.Text = "";
        _etyStaminaPoints.Text = "";
        _etyStaminaPointsMax.Text = "";
        _etyEnergyPoints.Text = "";
        _etyEnergyPointsMax.Text = "";
        _etyDefense.Text = "";
        _etyOffense.Text = "";
        _etyResistance.Text = "";
        _etyMagicPower.Text = "";
        _etySpeed.Text = "";
        _etySkillPoints.Text = "";
        break;
    }
  }
  public void UpdateTemplate()
  {
    bool isVisible = Templates.Count > 0;
    _lbTemplates.IsVisible = !isVisible;
    _btnSaveTemplate.IsVisible = isVisible;
    _btnClearTemplate.IsVisible = isVisible;
  }
  private void PckPartType_SelectedIndexChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    switch (_pckPartType.SelectedIndex)
    {
      case 0:// Player:
        ChangeVisiblity(42, 69, true);
        break;
      case 1:// Other:
        break;
      case 2:// NPC:
        break;
      case 3:// Item:
        break;
      case 4:// Object:
        break;
      case 5:// Map:
        ChangeVisiblity(42, 69, false);
        break;
      default:
        ChangeVisiblity(42, 69, false);
        break;
    }
  }
  private void ChangeVisiblity(int from, int to, bool visiblity)
  {
    for (int i = from; i <= to; i++)
    {
      ((View)_gridMain[i]).IsVisible = visiblity;
    }
    if (visiblity)
    {
      _etyHitPoints.Text = "0";
      _etyHitPointsMax.Text = "0";
      _etyMagicPoints.Text = "0";
      _etyMagicPointsMax.Text = "0";
      _etyStaminaPoints.Text = "0";
      _etyStaminaPointsMax.Text = "0";
      _etyEnergyPoints.Text = "0";
      _etyEnergyPointsMax.Text = "0";
      _etyOffense.Text = "0";
      _etyDefense.Text = "0";
      _etyResistance.Text = "0";
      _etyMagicPower.Text = "0";
      _etySpeed.Text = "0";
      _etySkillPoints.Text = "0";
    }
  }
  private void EtySkillPoints_TextChanged(object sender, TextChangedEventArgs e)
  {
    Entry entry = (Entry)sender;
    if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(e.NewTextValue))
    {
      entry.Text = "0";
      return;
    }
    if (int.TryParse(e.NewTextValue, out int value))
    {
      if (value < 0)
        entry.Text = "0";
    }
    else
      entry.Text = e.OldTextValue;
  }
}
//--------------------
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics.Text;
using Microsoft.Maui.Layouts;
using ReGame.SharedCode.Game;
using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Data;
using System.Reflection;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls
{
  public class UIWorldMap : Grid
  {
    private ObservableCollection<MapNavigationModel> listNavigations { get; set; }

    private Properies properies;
    private WorldMap worldMap;
    private UIStats uiStats;

    private bool isBrdNavRemoverActive;
    private int indexBrdNavRemover;
    private byte selectedIndex, indexSelectedNavBtn;
    private int selectedParts;
    
    public UIWorldMap()
    {
      worldMap = new();

      _InitializeComponent();
      _InitializeContents();

    }

    #region Components
    private FlexLayout _flxLocations;
    private ScrollView _scrvNavigations;
    private StackLayout _stlNavigations;
    private Grid _gridMapPart;
    private Border _brdNavRemover;

    /// <summary>
    /// Initialize the UIWorldMap components
    /// </summary>
    private void _InitializeComponent()
    {
      // Add 3 Rows to Grid
      RowDefinitions =
      [
        new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Auto) },
        new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Auto) },
        new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) },
      ];

      //Add a FlexLayout for managing the world and navigate back and forth between the worlds
      _flxLocations = new FlexLayout
      {
        AlignContent = FlexAlignContent.Start,
        AlignItems = FlexAlignItems.Start,
        JustifyContent = FlexJustify.SpaceAround,
        Wrap = FlexWrap.NoWrap,
        IsClippedToBounds = true,
        BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("#404040"),
        VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
      };
      // Create and add an Buttons to the FlexLayout
      Button btn;
      // Add '<' Button to  the right side
      btn = new Button()
      {
        Text = "<",
        FontSize = 19,
        Padding = new Thickness(0),
        Margin = new Thickness(0),
        BorderColor = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"),
        BorderWidth = 2,
        WidthRequest = 40
      }; _flxLocations.Children.Add(btn);
      for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++)
      {
        btn = new Button
        {
          Text = String.Format("{0:D2}", i),
          Padding = 0,
          Margin = 0,
          FontSize = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? 18 : 10,
          BorderColor = Color.FromArgb("#1E1E1E"),
          BorderWidth = 2,
          WidthRequest = 40
        }; _flxLocations.Children.Add(btn);
      }
      // Add '>' Button to  the left side
      btn = new Button()
      {
        Text = ">",
        FontSize = 19,
        Padding = new Thickness(0),
        Margin = new Thickness(0),
        BorderColor = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"),
        BorderWidth = 2,
        WidthRequest = 40
      }; _flxLocations.Children.Add(btn);
      // Add the _flxLocations to the Grid
      Children.Add(_flxLocations);

      // Add a ScrollView so we can scoll to the left and right of navigation buttons
      _scrvNavigations = new ScrollView
      {
        Orientation = ScrollOrientation.Horizontal,
        HorizontalScrollBarVisibility = ScrollBarVisibility.Never,
        VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center,
        BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("363636"),
        Margin = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? new Thickness(0) : new Thickness(0)
      };
      Grid.SetRow(_scrvNavigations, 1);
      // Add a StackLayout to navigate back and forth between the map
      _stlNavigations = new StackLayout
      {
        Orientation = StackOrientation.Horizontal,
        Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 0),
        IsClippedToBounds = true
      };
      // Create and add an ItemTemplate to the StackLayout
      DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate(() =>
      {
        var btn = new Button
        {
          Padding = new Thickness(4),
          Margin = new Thickness(0, 0, 2, 0),
          FontSize = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? 16 : 12,
          BorderColor = Color.FromArgb("CD853F"),
          BorderWidth = 2,
        };
        btn.SetBinding(Button.TextProperty, "Text");
        btn.Clicked += BtnNavigation_Clicked;
        btn.Unfocused += BtnNavigation_Unfocused;
        btn.GestureRecognizers.Add(new PointerGestureRecognizer());
        ((PointerGestureRecognizer)btn.GestureRecognizers[0]).PointerPressed += BtnNavigation_GR_PointerPressed;
        ((PointerGestureRecognizer)btn.GestureRecognizers[0]).PointerReleased += BtnNavigation_GR_PointerReleased;
        return btn;
      });
      BindableLayout.SetItemTemplate(_stlNavigations, dataTemplate);
      // Add the _stlNavigations to the _scrvNavigations
      _scrvNavigations.Content = _stlNavigations;
      // Add the _scrvNavigations to the Grid
      Children.Add(_scrvNavigations);

      // Add a Grid to display the world map
      _gridMapPart = new Grid
      {
        ColumnSpacing = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? 1 : 0.5,
        RowSpacing = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? 1 : 0.5,
      };
      // Add a border to the Grid
      Border border = new Border
      {
        Stroke = Color.FromArgb("CD853F"),
        StrokeThickness = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? 2 : 1
      };
      Grid.SetRow(border, 2);
      // Add the _gridMap to the Border
      border.Content = _gridMapPart;
      // Add the border to the _gridMapPart
      Children.Add(border);

      //   Message for removing mapParts tab
      _brdNavRemover = new Border
      {
        Stroke = Color.FromArgb("CD853F"),
        StrokeThickness = 2,
        HorizontalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start,
        VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start,
        HeightRequest = 60,
        Margin = new Thickness(0, 35, 0, 0),
        IsVisible = false
      };
      Grid.SetRow(_brdNavRemover, 1);
      Grid.SetRowSpan(_brdNavRemover, 2);
      // Add a Grid to display the message for removing mapParts tab
      var _gridMessage = new Grid
      {
        BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("77101010"),
        Padding = new Thickness(0, 0, 0, 2)
      };
      // Create and add an ItemTemplate to the Grid
      ColumnDefinitionCollection columnDefinitions =
      [
        new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(30) },
        new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(60) },
        new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(10) },
        new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(65) },
        new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(30) },
      ];
      _gridMessage.ColumnDefinitions = columnDefinitions;
      RowDefinitionCollection rowDefinitions =
      [
        new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) },
        new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) },
      ];
      _gridMessage.RowDefinitions = rowDefinitions;
      // Add the message to the _gridMessage
      var lblMessage = new Label
      {
        Text = "Do you like to remove this Part?",
        HorizontalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center,
        VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
      };
      Grid.SetColumn(lblMessage, 0);
      Grid.SetColumnSpan(lblMessage, 5);
      _gridMessage.Children.Add(lblMessage);
      // Add the buttons to the _gridMessage
      var btnCancelNavBtn = new Button
      {
        Text = "Cancel",
        Padding = new Thickness(7),
        VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
      };
      btnCancelNavBtn.Clicked += BtnCancelNavRemover_Clicked;
      Grid.SetColumn(btnCancelNavBtn, 1);
      Grid.SetRow(btnCancelNavBtn, 1);
      _gridMessage.Children.Add(btnCancelNavBtn);
      var btnRemoveNavBtn = new Button
      {
        Text = "Remove",
        Padding = new Thickness(7),
        VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Center
      };
      btnRemoveNavBtn.Clicked += BtnRemoveNavRemover_Clicked;
      Grid.SetColumn(btnRemoveNavBtn, 3);
      Grid.SetRow(btnRemoveNavBtn, 1);
      _gridMessage.Children.Add(btnRemoveNavBtn);
      // Add the _gridMessage to the _brdNavRemover
      _brdNavRemover.Content = _gridMessage;
      // Add the _brdNavRemover to the Grid
      Children.Add(_brdNavRemover);
    }
    /// <summary>
    /// Initialize contents of the UIWorldMap
    /// </summary>
    private void _InitializeContents()
    {
      // Add the contents to the _stlNavigations
      listNavigations = [new MapNavigationModel() { Text = "WorldMap" }];
      BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_stlNavigations, listNavigations);

      // Add the contents to the _gridMapPart
      byte cols = 11;
      byte rows = 5;
      for (int i = 0; i < cols; i++)
        _gridMapPart.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });
      for (int i = 0; i < rows; i++)
        _gridMapPart.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });

      Border border;
      worldMap.World.Add(new IMapPart[cols * rows]);
      int index = -1;
      for (int j = 0; j < rows; j++)
      {
        for (int i = 0; i < cols; i++)
        {
          worldMap.World[0][++index] = new MapPartModel() { PartType = MapPartType.Empty, Text = "", Description = "", BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("282828"), Stroke = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"), TextColor = Color.FromArgb("FFFFFF"), IsEnable = true, IsVisible = true, Parent = -1, Child = -1, ColumnRow = [(byte)i, (byte)j] };
          var label = new Label
          {
            FontSize = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? 12 : 8,
            HorizontalTextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center,
            VerticalTextAlignment = TextAlignment.Center,
            Text = worldMap.World[0][index].Text,
            TextColor = worldMap.World[0][index].TextColor,
          };

          border = new Border
          {
            StrokeThickness = DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI ? 4 : 2,
            Stroke = worldMap.World[0][index].Stroke,
            BackgroundColor = worldMap.World[0][index].BackgroundColor,
          };
          Grid.SetColumn(border, worldMap.World[0][index].ColumnRow[0]); // i
          Grid.SetRow(border, worldMap.World[0][index].ColumnRow[1]); // j
          border.Content = label;
          _gridMapPart.Children.Add(border);
          border.GestureRecognizers.Add(new TapGestureRecognizer());
          ((TapGestureRecognizer)border.GestureRecognizers[0]).Tapped += MapPart_GR_Tapped;
          border.GestureRecognizers.Add(new TapGestureRecognizer());
          ((TapGestureRecognizer)border.GestureRecognizers[1]).Tapped += MapPart_GR_DoubleTapped;
          ((TapGestureRecognizer)border.GestureRecognizers[1]).NumberOfTapsRequired = 2;
          border.GestureRecognizers.Add(new TapGestureRecognizer());
          ((TapGestureRecognizer)border.GestureRecognizers[2]).Tapped += MapPart_GR_RightTapped;
          ((TapGestureRecognizer)border.GestureRecognizers[2]).Buttons = ButtonsMask.Secondary;
        }
      }
    }
    #endregion

    #region Stats Events
    public void SetStats(UIStats uiStats)
    {
      this.uiStats = uiStats;
    }
    #endregion

    #region Properties Events
    public void SetProperties(Properies view)
    {
      properies = view;
      properies.UpdateMapPartEvent += Properies_UpdateMapPartEventArs;
      properies.SaveMapPartEvent += Properies_SaveMapPartEvent;
      properies.LoadMapPartEvent += Properies_LoadMapPartEvent;
      properies.ClearMapPartEvent += Properies_ClearMapPartEvent;
    }
    private void Properies_UpdateMapPartEventArs(IMapPart mapPart)
    {
      Properies_UpdateMapPartEventArs(mapPart, selectedIndex);
    }
    public void Properies_UpdateMapPartEventArs(IMapPart mapPart, byte selectedIndex)
    {
      worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex] = (IMapPart)mapPart.Clone();

      Border border; Label label;
      border = (Border)_gridMapPart.Children[selectedIndex];
      label = (Label)border.Content;

      border.Stroke = mapPart.Stroke;
      label.BackgroundColor = mapPart.BackgroundColor;
      label.TextColor = mapPart.TextColor;
      label.Text = mapPart.Text;
    }
    private void Properies_SaveMapPartEvent(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
      //TODO: 
      worldMap.Templates = properies.Templates;
      worldMap.SaveAll();
      //worldMap.WriteMapParts(mapParts, (short)_gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count, (short)_gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count);
      Activities.Instance.InsertData("Users", "Name,Info,Level,Gold,Age,XP,Location,Character,Tutorial", "@Name,@Info,@Level,@Gold,@Age,@XP,@Location,@Character,@Tutorial", new object[]
      {
        "Name","Info",1,1000,27,190,"Location",worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex],false
      });
    }
    private void Properies_LoadMapPartEvent(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {
      properies.Templates.Clear();
      //TODO: 
      worldMap.LoadMap();

      for (int i = 0; i < worldMap.Templates.Count; i++)
      {
        properies.Templates.Add(worldMap.Templates[i]);
      }
      properies.UpdateTemplate();

      selectedParts = 0;
      UpdateContent();
    }
    private void Properies_ClearMapPartEvent(object? sender, EventArgs e)
    {

    }
    #endregion

    #region Events Navigations throw Maps
    private void BtnNavigation_GR_PointerPressed(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
    {
      int index = _stlNavigations.IndexOf((View)sender);
      if (index == 0) return;
      if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible && index != indexBrdNavRemover)
      {
        indexBrdNavRemover = index;
        BtnNavRemover_Focused(sender);
      }
      else if (!_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
      {
        indexBrdNavRemover = index;
        if (!isBrdNavRemoverActive && !_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
          Dispatcher.DispatchDelayed(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(.5), () =>
          {
            if (isBrdNavRemoverActive)
              BtnNavRemover_Focused(sender);
          });
        isBrdNavRemoverActive = true;
      }
    }
    private void BtnNavigation_GR_PointerReleased(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
    {
      isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
    }
    private void BtnNavigation_Unfocused(object sender, FocusEventArgs e)
    {
      isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
    }
    private void BtnNavigation_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
    {
      if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
        BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();

      indexSelectedNavBtn = (byte)_stlNavigations.IndexOf((View)sender);

      int indexNav = indexSelectedNavBtn;
      indexNav = listNavigations[indexNav].Parent;
      if (indexNav != selectedParts)
      {
        selectedParts = indexNav;
        UpdateContent();
      }
      for (int i = indexSelectedNavBtn + 1; i < listNavigations.Count;)
      {
        listNavigations.RemoveAt(i);
      }
    }

    private void MapPart_GR_Tapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
      if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
        BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();

      selectedIndex = (byte)_gridMapPart.Children.IndexOf((Border)sender);
      IMapPart mapPart = worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex];
      properies.SetMapPart(mapPart);

      if (mapPart is CharacterModel)
        uiStats.UpdateStats(((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats, 1);
    }
    private void MapPart_GR_DoubleTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
      if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
        BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();

      selectedIndex = (byte)_gridMapPart.Children.IndexOf((Border)sender);

      int index;
      
      if (worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex].Child > 0)
      {
        selectedParts = worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex].Child;
        listNavigations.Add(new() { Text = worldMap.World[listNavigations[listNavigations.Count - 1].Parent][selectedIndex].Text, Parent = selectedParts });
      }
      else
      {
        index = -1;
        IMapPart[] mapParts = new MapPartModel[_gridMapPart.ColumnDefinitions.Count * _gridMapPart.RowDefinitions.Count];
        for (int j = 0; j < _gridMapPart.RowDefinitions.Count; j++)
        {
          for (int i = 0; i < _gridMapPart.ColumnDefinitions.Count; i++)
          {
            mapParts[++index] = new MapPartModel()
            {
              PartType = MapPartType.Empty,
              Text = "",
              Description = "",
              BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("282828"),
              Stroke = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"),
              TextColor = Color.FromArgb("FFFFFF"),
              IsEnable = true,
              IsVisible = true,
              Parent = selectedParts,
              Child = -1,
              ColumnRow = [(byte)i, (byte)j]
            };
          }
        }
        
        worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex].Child = worldMap.World.Count;
        listNavigations.Add(new() { Text = worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex].Text, Parent = worldMap.World.Count });
        selectedParts = worldMap.World.Count;
        worldMap.World.Add(mapParts);
      }
      UpdateContent();
    }
    private void MapPart_GR_RightTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
    {
      int index = _gridMapPart.Children.IndexOf((Border)sender);

      IMapPart mapPart = worldMap.World[selectedParts][index];
      mapPart.PartType = properies.MapPart.PartType;
      mapPart.Stroke = properies.MapPart.Stroke;
      mapPart.BackgroundColor = properies.MapPart.BackgroundColor;
      mapPart.TextColor = properies.MapPart.TextColor;
      if(mapPart.PartType == MapPartType.Player)
      {
        if (mapPart is not CharacterModel)
          mapPart = new CharacterModel(mapPart);
        ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Race = new RaceModel() { Name = "Human", Description = "Description of Humans" };
        ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats = ((CharacterModel)properies.MapPart).Stats;
        properies.SetMapPart(mapPart);
      }

      Border border; Label label;
      border = (Border)_gridMapPart.Children[index];
      label = (Label)border.Content;

      label.TextColor = properies.MapPart.TextColor;
      label.BackgroundColor = properies.MapPart.BackgroundColor;
      border.Stroke = properies.MapPart.Stroke;
    }

    private void UpdateContent()
    {
      Border border; Label label;
      for (int i = 0; i < worldMap.World[selectedParts].Length; i++)
      {
        border = (Border)_gridMapPart.Children[i];
        label = (Label)border.Content;
        border.Stroke = worldMap.World[selectedParts][i].Stroke;
        label.BackgroundColor = worldMap.World[selectedParts][i].BackgroundColor;
        label.TextColor = worldMap.World[selectedParts][i].TextColor;
        label.Text = worldMap.World[selectedParts][i].Text;
      }
    }
    public IMapPart GetData(byte index)
    {
      return worldMap.World[selectedParts][index];
    }
    #endregion

    #region Events NavRemover
    private async void BtnNavRemover_Focused(object sender)
    {
      isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
      _brdNavRemover.IsVisible = true;
      _brdNavRemover.Opacity = 0;
      _brdNavRemover.Scale = 0;
      _brdNavRemover.TranslationX = ((View)sender).X;
      await _brdNavRemover.FadeTo(1, 150);
      await _brdNavRemover.ScaleTo(1.1, 150);
      await _brdNavRemover.ScaleTo(1, 150);
    }
    private void BtnNavRemover_Unfocused(object sender, FocusEventArgs e)
    {
      isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
    }
    private async void BrdNavRemover_Unfocused()
    {
      isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
      await _brdNavRemover.FadeTo(0, 150);
      _brdNavRemover.IsVisible = false;
      _brdNavRemover.Opacity = 0;
    }
    private void BtnCancelNavRemover_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
    {
      BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();
    }
    private void BtnRemoveNavRemover_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
    {
      BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();
    }
    #endregion
  }
}

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls;

public partial class UIBattleSystem : Grid
{

  public UIBattleSystem()
	{
		InitializeComponent();

	}

  byte selectedBtn9;
  private void Buttons9_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    View view = (View)sender;
    int col = (int)view.GetValue(Grid.ColumnProperty);
    int row = (int)view.GetValue(Grid.RowProperty);
    Grid.SetColumn(_vboxBg, col);
    Grid.SetRow(_vboxBg, row);
    _vboxBg.IsVisible = true;

    selectedBtn9 = (byte)_gridBtns9.IndexOf(view);
  }
}

public class UIBehaviours : Grid
{
  private ObservableCollection<SIM> listIntractiveBtns { get; set; }
  private ObservableCollection<SIM> listSystemItems { get; set; }
  private ObservableCollection<SIM> listNSystemItems { get; set; }
  private short selectedBtns9;

  private UIWorldMap uiWorldMap;

  public UIBehaviours()
  {
    _InitializeComponent();
    _InitializeContents();
  }

  #region Components

  private FlexLayout _flxIntractiveButtons, _flxSystemItems, _flxNSysten;
  private Grid _gridBtns9;

  /// <summary>
  /// Initialize the UIBehaviours components
  /// </summary>
  private void _InitializeComponent()
  {
    Padding = new(2);
    BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("DB8E43");

    // Add 7 columns
    ColumnDefinitions =
    [
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(.01, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(.01, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(.01, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
    ];

    // Column 0
    Grid grid = new()
    {
      Padding = new(4),
      BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("303030")
    };
    grid.RowDefinitions =
    [
      new(new GridLength(.7, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new(new GridLength(.4, GridUnitType.Auto))
    ];

    _label = new() { LineBreakMode = LineBreakMode.WordWrap };
    grid.Add(_label);

    _flxIntractiveButtons = new()
    {
      AlignContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignContent.Center,
      AlignItems = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignItems.Center,
      JustifyContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexJustify.Center,
      Wrap = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexWrap.Wrap,
    };
    DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate(() =>
    {
      Grid grid = new();

      BoxView boxView = new()
      {
        BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("B03030"),
        CornerRadius = 4,
      };
      grid.Add(boxView);

      Button btn = new();
      btn.SetBinding(Button.TextProperty, "Text");
      btn.Clicked += BtnIntractive_Clicked;
      grid.Add(btn);

      return grid;
    });
    BindableLayout.SetItemTemplate(_flxIntractiveButtons, dataTemplate);

    ScrollView _scrollView = new();
    Grid.SetRow(_scrollView, 1);
    _scrollView.Content = _flxIntractiveButtons;
    grid.Add(_scrollView);
    Add(grid);

    // Column 1

    // Column 2
    _gridBtns9 = new Grid();
    _gridBtns9.BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("232323");
    _gridBtns9.ColumnDefinitions =
    [
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new ColumnDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
    ];
    _gridBtns9.RowDefinitions =
    [
      new RowDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new RowDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
      new RowDefinition(new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star)),
    ];

    //_boxVBtn9 = new BoxView();
    //_boxVBtn9.BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("307000");
    //_boxVBtn9.IsVisible = false;
    //_gridBtns9.Add(_boxVBtn9);

    ImageButton _imageButton;
    for (int j = 0; j < _gridBtns9.RowDefinitions.Count; j++)
    {
      for (int i = 0; i < _gridBtns9.ColumnDefinitions.Count; i++)
      {
        _imageButton = new ImageButton();
        _imageButton.Source = new FontImageSource()
        {
          FontFamily = "SegMDL2",
          Glyph = "\uE72E",
        };
        _imageButton.Clicked += Buttons9_Clicked;
        Grid.SetColumn(_imageButton, i);
        Grid.SetRow(_imageButton, j);
        _gridBtns9.Add(_imageButton);
      }
    }
    ((FontImageSource)((ImageButton)_gridBtns9[0]).Source).Glyph = "\uE785";
    Grid.SetColumn(_gridBtns9, 2);
    Add(_gridBtns9);

    // Column 3

    // Column 4
    _flxSystemItems = new()
    {
      AlignContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignContent.Start,
      AlignItems = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignItems.Center,
      JustifyContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexJustify.Start,
      Wrap = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexWrap.Wrap,
    };
    dataTemplate = new DataTemplate(() =>
    {
      Button btn = new();
      btn.Margin = new Thickness(1, 2, 1, 0);
      btn.SetBinding(Button.TextProperty, "Text");
      btn.Clicked += BtnSystemItems_Clicked;
      return btn;
    });
    BindableLayout.SetItemTemplate(_flxSystemItems, dataTemplate);

    _scrollView = new();
    _scrollView.BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("303030");
    Grid.SetColumn(_scrollView, 4);
    _scrollView.Content = _flxSystemItems;
    Add(_scrollView);

    // Column 5

    // Column 6
    _flxNSysten = new()
    {
      AlignContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignContent.Start,
      AlignItems = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignItems.Center,
      JustifyContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexJustify.Start,
      Wrap = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexWrap.Wrap,
    };
    dataTemplate = new DataTemplate(() =>
    {
      Button btn = new();
      btn.Margin = new Thickness(1, 2, 1, 0);
      btn.SetBinding(Button.TextProperty, "Text");
      btn.Clicked += BtnNSystemItems_Clicked;
      return btn;
    });
    BindableLayout.SetItemTemplate(_flxNSysten, dataTemplate);

    _scrollView = new();
    _scrollView.BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("303030");
    Grid.SetColumn(_scrollView, 6);
    _scrollView.Content = _flxNSysten;
    Add(_scrollView);
  }

  /// <summary>
  /// Initialize contents of the UIBehaviours
  /// </summary>
  private void _InitializeContents()
  {
    // Add the contents to the _flxIntractiveButtons
    listIntractiveBtns = new();
    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_flxIntractiveButtons, listIntractiveBtns);

    // Add the contents to the _flxSystemItems
    listSystemItems = new();
    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_flxSystemItems, listSystemItems);

    listNSystemItems = new();
    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_flxNSysten, listNSystemItems);


    // Load Profile
    //Profile.Instance.LoadData();

  }
  #endregion

  #region Events
  private void BtnIntractive_Clicked(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
  }
  private void Buttons9_Clicked(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    //((FontImageSource)((ImageButton)_gridBtns9[0]).Source).Glyph = "\uE785";
    selectedBtns9 = (byte)_gridBtns9.IndexOf((IView)sender);

    listSystemItems.Clear();
    listNSystemItems.Clear();
    switch (selectedBtns9)
    {
      case 0: // Offence
        listSystemItems.Insert(0, new SIM("+"));
        break;
    }
  }
  private void BtnSystemItems_Clicked(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    byte index = (byte)_flxSystemItems.IndexOf((IView)sender);

    listNSystemItems.Clear();
    switch (index)
    {
      case 0: // Offence
        listNSystemItems.Add(new SIM("Fire Ball"));
        listNSystemItems.Add(new SIM("Lightning"));
        listNSystemItems.Add(new SIM("Arrow Air"));
        break;
    }

  }
  private void BtnNSystemItems_Clicked(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    byte index = (byte)_flxNSysten.IndexOf((IView)sender);
    //Select_Skill(listNSystemItems[index].Text);
    listNSystemItems.Clear();

  }
  #endregion

  #region Custom Events
  public void SetUIWorldMap(UIWorldMap uiWorldMap)
  {
    this.uiWorldMap = uiWorldMap;
    
  }
  private void Select_Skill(string text)
  {
    _flxNSysten = new()
    {
      AlignContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignContent.Start,
      AlignItems = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexAlignItems.Center,
      JustifyContent = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexJustify.Start,
      Wrap = Microsoft.Maui.Layouts.FlexWrap.Wrap,
    };
    DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate(() =>
    {
      Button btn = new();
      btn.Margin = new Thickness(1, 2, 1, 0);
      btn.SetBinding(Button.TextProperty, "Text");
      btn.Clicked += BtnNSystemItems_Clicked;
      return btn;
    });
    BindableLayout.SetItemTemplate(_flxNSysten, dataTemplate);
  }

  #endregion
}

              <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="TapGR_Tapped"/>
              <!--<TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="PointerGRMapPart_Tapped" Buttons="Secondary" />-->
              <TapGestureRecognizer Tapped="DoubleTapGR_MapPart" NumberOfTapsRequired="2"/>
            </Border.GestureRecognizers>
          </Border>
        </DataTemplate>
      </BindableLayout.ItemTemplate>
    </Grid>
  </Border>

  <!-- Message for removing mapParts tab -->
  <Border Grid.Row="1" Grid.RowSpan="2" x:Name="_brdNavRemover" Stroke="#CD853F" StrokeThickness="2" HorizontalOptions="Start" VerticalOptions="Start" HeightRequest="60" Margin="0,35,0,0" IsVisible="False">

using ReGame.SharedCode.Game;
using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Reflection;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls;

public partial class UIMap : Grid
{
  private ObservableCollection<IMapPart> mapParts { get; set; }
  private ObservableCollection<MapNavigationModel> listNavigations { get; set; }
  private ObservableCollection<int> listLocations { get; set; }

  private WorldMap worldMap;
  private Properies properies;
  private MapPartModel mapPartTemplate;
  private bool isBrdNavRemoverActive;
  private int indexBrdNavRemover;
  private int selectedIndex, selectedParts, indexSelectedNavBtn;

  public UIMap()
  {
    worldMap = new();

    InitializeComponent();

    //    int columns, rows;
    //#if WINDOWS || MACCATALYST
    //    columns = 11; rows = 5;
    //#elif ANDROID || IOS
    //    columns = 5; rows = 11;
    //#else
    //    columns = 11; rows = 5;
    //#endif

    //    for (int i = 0; i < columns; i++)
    //    {
    //      _gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition() { Width = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });
    //    }
    //    for (int i = 0; i < rows; i++)
    //    {
    //      _gridMap.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition() { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });
    //    }
    //    DeviceDisplay.MainDisplayInfoChanged += DeviceDisplay_MainDisplayInfoChanged;

    mapParts = new();
    listNavigations = new();
    listLocations = new();

    for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++)
    {
      listLocations.Add(i);
    }
    int index = -1;
    for (int j = 0; j < _gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count; j++)
    {
      for (int i = 0; i < _gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count; i++)
      {
        mapParts.Add(new MapPartModel() { PartType = MapPartType.Empty, Text = "", Description = "", BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("282828"), Stroke = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"), TextColor = Color.FromArgb("FFFFFF"), IsEnable = true, IsVisible = true, Parent = -1, Child = -1 });
        mapParts[++index].ColumnRow = [(byte)i, (byte)j];
      }
    }

    mapPartTemplate = new() { BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("282828"), Stroke = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"), TextColor = Color.FromArgb("FFFFFF"), IsEnable = true, IsVisible = true, Parent = -1, Child = -1 };
    mapPartTemplate.ColumnRow = [0, 0];

    listNavigations.Add(new MapNavigationModel() { Text = "WorldMap" });

    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_stlNavigations, listNavigations);
    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_flxLocations, listLocations);
    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_gridMap, mapParts);
    //TODO:
    //worldMap.ColumnsRows.Add([(byte)_gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count, (byte)_gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count]);
    worldMap.World.Add(mapParts.ToArray());

    // Add Two Extra Label to Locations
    _flxLocations.Children.Insert(0, new Button() { Text = "<", Padding = new Thickness(0), Margin = new Thickness(0), BorderColor = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"), BorderWidth = 2, WidthRequest = 40 });
    _flxLocations.Children.Insert(_flxLocations.Children.Count, new Button() { Text = ">", Padding = new Thickness(0), Margin = new Thickness(0), BorderColor = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"), BorderWidth = 2, WidthRequest = 40 });

    if (DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.WinUI || DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.macOS)
    {
      ((Button)_flxLocations.Children[0]).FontSize = 19;
      ((Button)_flxLocations.Children[_flxLocations.Children.Count - 1]).FontSize = 19;
    }
    else if (DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.Android || DeviceInfo.Platform == DevicePlatform.iOS)
    {
      ((Button)_flxLocations.Children[0]).FontSize = 9;
      ((Button)_flxLocations.Children[_flxLocations.Children.Count - 1]).FontSize = 9;
    }

    TapGestureRecognizer tapGesture = new();
    tapGesture.Tapped += TapGRMapPart_RightTapped;
    tapGesture.Buttons = ButtonsMask.Secondary;
    for (int i = 0; i < mapParts.Count; i++)
    {
      ((Border)_gridMap[i]).GestureRecognizers.Add(tapGesture);
    }
  }

  private void DeviceDisplay_MainDisplayInfoChanged(object? sender, DisplayInfoChangedEventArgs e)
  {

  }

  private async void ScrollView_Scrolled(object sender, ScrolledEventArgs e)
  {
  }

  private void PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerEntered(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    ((VisualElement)sender).BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("4C4C4C");
  }
  private void PointerGestureRecognizer_PointerExited(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    ((VisualElement)sender).BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("3C3C3C");
  }

  private void BtnNavigation_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
      BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();

    indexSelectedNavBtn = _stlNavigations.IndexOf((View)sender);
    UpdateMap_SelectedNav(indexSelectedNavBtn);
  }
  private void UpdateMap_SelectedNav(int index)
  {
    indexSelectedNavBtn = index;
    int indexNav = indexSelectedNavBtn;
    indexNav = listNavigations[indexNav].Parent;
    if (indexNav != selectedParts)
    {
      selectedParts = indexNav;
      UpdateMapParts(selectedParts);
    }
    for (int i = indexSelectedNavBtn + 1; i < listNavigations.Count;)
    {
      listNavigations.RemoveAt(i);
    }
  }

  private void NavPointerGR_PointerPressed(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    int index = _stlNavigations.IndexOf((View)sender);
    if (index == 0) return;
    if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible && index != indexBrdNavRemover)
    {
      indexBrdNavRemover = index;
      BtnNav_Focused(sender);
    }
    else if (!_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
    {
      indexBrdNavRemover = index;
      if (!isBrdNavRemoverActive && !_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
        Dispatcher.DispatchDelayed(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(.5), () =>
        {
          if (isBrdNavRemoverActive)
            BtnNav_Focused(sender);
        });
      isBrdNavRemoverActive = true;
    }
  }
  private void NavPointerGR_PointerReleased(object sender, PointerEventArgs e)
  {
    isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
  }
  private async void BtnNav_Focused(object sender)
  {
    isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
    _brdNavRemover.IsVisible = true;
    _brdNavRemover.Opacity = 0;
    _brdNavRemover.Scale = 0;
    _brdNavRemover.TranslationX = ((View)sender).X;
    await _brdNavRemover.FadeTo(1, 150);
    await _brdNavRemover.ScaleTo(1.1, 150);
    await _brdNavRemover.ScaleTo(1, 150);
  }
  private void BtnNav_Unfocused(object sender, FocusEventArgs e)
  {
    isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
  }
  private async void BrdNavRemover_Unfocused()
  {
    isBrdNavRemoverActive = false;
    await _brdNavRemover.FadeTo(0, 150);
    _brdNavRemover.IsVisible = false;
    _brdNavRemover.Opacity = 0;
  }
  private void BtnCancelNavBtn_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();
  }
  private void BtnRemoveNavBtn_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();
  }

  public void SetProperties(Properies view)
  {
    properies = view;
    properies.UpdateMapPartEvent += Properies_UpdateMapPartEventArs;
    properies.SaveMapPartEvent += Properies_SaveMapPartEvent;
    properies.LoadMapPartEvent += Properies_LoadMapPartEvent;
    properies.ClearMapPartEvent += Properies_ClearMapPartEvent;
  }
  private void Properies_SaveMapPartEvent(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    //TODO: 
    worldMap.Templates = properies.Templates;
    worldMap.SaveAll();
    //worldMap.WriteMapParts(mapParts, (short)_gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count, (short)_gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count);
  }
  private void Properies_LoadMapPartEvent(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    properies.Templates.Clear();
    //TODO: 
    worldMap.LoadMap();

    for (int i = 0; i < worldMap.Templates.Count; i++)
    {
      properies.Templates.Add(worldMap.Templates[i]);
    }
    
    //properies.LabelTemplated(worldMap.Templates.Count < 0);

    //((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[31]).IsEnabled = true; // _btnAdd
    //((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[32]).IsEnabled = worldMap.Templates.Count > 0; // _btnSave
    //((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[33]).IsEnabled = true; // _btnLoad
    //((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[34]).IsEnabled = worldMap.Templates.Count > 0; // _btnClear

    UpdateMapParts(0);
  }
  private void Properies_ClearMapPartEvent(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {

  }
  private void Properies_UpdateMapPartEventArs(IMapPart mapPart)
  {
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].Parent = mapPart.Parent;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].Child = mapPart.Child;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].Text = mapPart.Text;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].Description = mapPart.Description;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].ColumnRow = [mapPart.ColumnRow[0], mapPart.ColumnRow[1]];
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].Stroke = mapPart.Stroke;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].BackgroundColor = mapPart.BackgroundColor;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].TextColor = mapPart.TextColor;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].IsEnable = mapPart.IsEnable;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].IsVisible = mapPart.IsVisible;
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex].PartType = mapPart.PartType;

    this.mapParts[selectedIndex] = (IMapPart)mapPart.Clone();
    //this.mapParts[selectedIndex] = mapPart;
    worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex] = this.mapParts[selectedIndex];

    //Border border; Label label;
    //border = (Border)_gridMap.Children[selectedIndex];
    //label = (Label)border.Content;

    //label.Text = mapPart.Text;
    //label.TextColor = mapPart.TextColor;
    //label.BackgroundColor = mapPart.BackgroundColor;
    //border.Stroke = mapPart.Stroke;
    //border.IsEnabled = mapPart.IsEnable;
    //border.IsVisible = mapPart.IsVisible;
  }

  private void TapGRMapPart_RightTapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
  {
    mapPartTemplate.Stroke = mapParts[selectedIndex].Stroke;
    mapPartTemplate.BackgroundColor = mapParts[selectedIndex].BackgroundColor;
    mapPartTemplate.TextColor = mapParts[selectedIndex].TextColor;
    mapPartTemplate.PartType = MapPartType.Map;

    int index = _gridMap.Children.IndexOf((Border)sender);

    mapParts[index].Stroke = mapPartTemplate.Stroke;
    mapParts[index].BackgroundColor = mapPartTemplate.BackgroundColor;
    mapParts[index].TextColor = mapPartTemplate.TextColor;
    mapParts[index].PartType = mapPartTemplate.PartType;
    //TODO: 
    worldMap.World[selectedParts][index] = mapParts[index];

    Border border; Label label;
    border = (Border)_gridMap.Children[index];
    label = (Label)border.Content;

    label.TextColor = mapPartTemplate.TextColor;
    label.BackgroundColor = mapPartTemplate.BackgroundColor;
    border.Stroke = mapPartTemplate.Stroke;
  }
  private void TapGR_Tapped(object? sender, TappedEventArgs e)
  {
    if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
      BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();

    selectedIndex = _gridMap.Children.IndexOf((Border)sender);
    GetMapPart(mapParts[selectedIndex]);
  }
  public void GetMapPart(IMapPart mapPart)
  {
    ((Label)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[3]).Text = mapPart.Parent.ToString(); // Prent
    ((Label)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[4]).Text = mapPart.Child.ToString(); // Child
    ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[6]).Text = mapPart.Text; // Text
    ((Editor)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[8]).Text = mapPart.Description; // Description
    ((Label)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[10]).Text = mapPart.ColumnRow[0].ToString(); // Column
    ((Label)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[11]).Text = mapPart.ColumnRow[1].ToString(); // Row

    properies.MapPart.Stroke = mapPart.Stroke;
    properies.MapPart.BackgroundColor = mapPart.BackgroundColor;
    properies.MapPart.TextColor = mapPart.TextColor;

    // Border
    ((Button)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[26]).BorderColor = ((SolidColorBrush)mapPart.Stroke).Color;
    // BackgroundColor
    ((Button)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[26]).BackgroundColor = mapPart.BackgroundColor;
    // TextColor
    ((Button)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[26]).TextColor = mapPart.TextColor;

    // Stroke
    ((Button)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[14]).BackgroundColor = ((SolidColorBrush)mapPart.Stroke).Color;
    // BackgroundColor
    ((Button)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[17]).BackgroundColor = mapPart.BackgroundColor;
    // TextColor
    ((Button)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[20]).BackgroundColor = mapPart.TextColor;

    ((CheckBox)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[23]).IsChecked = mapPart.IsEnable; // IsEnable
    ((CheckBox)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[25]).IsChecked = mapPart.IsVisible; // IsVisible

    ((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[31]).IsEnabled = true; // _btnAdd
    //((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[32]).IsEnabled = false; // _btnSave
    //((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[33]).IsEnabled = true; // _btnLoad
    //((VisualElement)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[34]).IsEnabled = false; // _btnClear

    ((Picker)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[41]).SelectedIndex = (int)mapPart.PartType - 1;
    switch (mapPart.PartType)
    {
      case MapPartType.Player:
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[43]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.HitPointsMax.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[45]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.MagicPointsMax.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[47]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.StaminaPointsMax.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[49]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.EnergyPointsMax.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[51]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.Offense.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[53]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.Defense.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[55]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.Resistance.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[57]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.MagicPower.ToString();
        ((Entry)((Grid)properies.Content).Children[59]).Text = ((CharacterModel)mapPart).Stats.SkillPoints.ToString();
        break;
      case MapPartType.Other:
        break;
      case MapPartType.NPC:
        break;
      case MapPartType.Item:
        break;
      case MapPartType.Object:
        break;
      case MapPartType.Map:
        break;
    }
  }
  private void DoubleTapGR_MapPart(object sender, TappedEventArgs e)
  {
    if (_brdNavRemover.IsVisible)
      BrdNavRemover_Unfocused();

    selectedIndex = _gridMap.Children.IndexOf((Border)sender);
    int index;
    IMapPart[] mapParts;
    if (this.mapParts[selectedIndex].Child > 0)
    {
      selectedParts = this.mapParts[selectedIndex].Child;
      // Orginal Code
      listNavigations.Add(new() { Text = worldMap.World[listNavigations[listNavigations.Count - 1].Parent][selectedIndex].Text, Parent = selectedParts });
      mapParts = worldMap.World[selectedParts];
    }
    else
    {
      index = -1;
      mapParts = new MapPartModel[_gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count * _gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count];
      for (int j = 0; j < _gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count; j++)
      {
        for (int i = 0; i < _gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count; i++)
        {
          mapParts[++index] = new MapPartModel()
          {
            PartType = MapPartType.Empty,
            Text = "",
            Description = "",
            BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("282828"),
            Stroke = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"),
            TextColor = Color.FromArgb("FFFFFF"),
            IsEnable = true,
            IsVisible = true,
            Parent = selectedParts,
            Child = -1,
            ColumnRow = [(byte)i, (byte)j]
          };
        }
      }
      //TODO: 
      worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex].Child = worldMap.World.Count;
      listNavigations.Add(new() { Text = worldMap.World[selectedParts][selectedIndex].Text, Parent = worldMap.World.Count });
      selectedParts = worldMap.World.Count;

      //worldMap.ColumnsRows.Add([(byte)_gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count, (byte)_gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count]);
      worldMap.World.Add(mapParts);
    }

    UpdateMapParts(selectedParts);
  }

  private void UpdateMapParts(int index)
  {
    // Load the map parts from the world map
    //worldMap.ColumnsRows[index] = [cols, rows];
    //_gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Clear();
    //_gridMap.RowDefinitions.Clear();
    //for (int i = 0; i < cols; i++)
    //  _gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Add(new ColumnDefinition { Width = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });
    //for (int i = 0; i < rows; i++)
    //  _gridMap.RowDefinitions.Add(new RowDefinition { Height = new GridLength(1, GridUnitType.Star) });

    //TapGestureRecognizer tapGesture = new();
    //tapGesture.Tapped += TapGRMapPart_RightTapped;
    //tapGesture.Buttons = ButtonsMask.Secondary;
    //mapParts.Clear();

    Border border; Label label;
    int index2 = -1;
    for (int j = 0; j < _gridMap.RowDefinitions.Count; j++)
    {
      for (int i = 0; i < _gridMap.ColumnDefinitions.Count; i++)
      {
        index2++;
        switch (worldMap.World[index][index2].PartType)
        {
          case MapPartType.Player:
            //mapParts[index2] = new CharacterModel();
            ((CharacterModel)mapParts[index2]).Race = ((CharacterModel)worldMap.World[index][index2]).Race;
            ((CharacterModel)mapParts[index2]).Stats = ((CharacterModel)worldMap.World[index][index2]).Stats;
            break;
          case MapPartType.Other:
            break;
          case MapPartType.NPC:
            break;
          case MapPartType.Item:
            break;
          case MapPartType.Object:
            break;
          case MapPartType.Map:
            //mapParts[index2] = new MapPartModel();
            break;
        }
        //mapParts[index2] = worldMap.World[index][index2];
        mapParts[index2].PartType = worldMap.World[index][index2].PartType;
        mapParts[index2].Parent = worldMap.World[index][index2].Parent;
        mapParts[index2].Child = worldMap.World[index][index2].Child;
        mapParts[index2].Text = worldMap.World[index][index2].Text;
        mapParts[index2].Description = worldMap.World[index][index2].Description;
        mapParts[index2].ColumnRow = [worldMap.World[index][index2].ColumnRow[0], worldMap.World[index][index2].ColumnRow[1]];
        mapParts[index2].Stroke = worldMap.World[index][index2].Stroke;
        mapParts[index2].BackgroundColor = worldMap.World[index][index2].BackgroundColor;
        mapParts[index2].TextColor = worldMap.World[index][index2].TextColor;
        mapParts[index2].IsEnable = worldMap.World[index][index2].IsEnable;
        mapParts[index2].IsVisible = worldMap.World[index][index2].IsVisible;

        //((Border)_gridMap[index2]).GestureRecognizers.Add(tapGesture);
        border = (Border)_gridMap.Children[index2];
        label = (Label)border.Content;
        label.Text = mapParts[index2].Text;
        label.TextColor = mapParts[index2].TextColor;
        label.BackgroundColor = mapParts[index2].BackgroundColor;
        border.Stroke = mapParts[index2].Stroke;
      }
    }
  }
}

<Grid xmlns="http://schemas.microsoft.com/dotnet/2021/maui"
