  <Label Grid.Column="1" x:Name="lb2" Text="999999 / 999999" FontAttributes="Bold" TextColor="LightGreen" VerticalTextAlignment="Center" HorizontalTextAlignment="Center"/>

  <Border Grid.Column="2" Grid.ColumnSpan="3" x:Name="outerLine" Padding="1" Stroke="Lime" StrokeThickness="2" Margin="4,0,0,0">

using Google.Protobuf.WellKnownTypes;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls;
public enum UIRectMode
{
  None = 0,
  /// <summary>Default mode.</summary>
  Default = 1,
  /// <summary>Mode for displaying a value.</summary>
  Value = 2,
  /// <summary>Mode for displaying a percentage.</summary>
  Percentage = 3,
}
public partial class UIRect : Grid
{
  /// <summary>Bindable property for <see cref="DisplayMode"/>.</summary>
  public static readonly BindableProperty DisplayModeProperty =
    BindableProperty.Create("DisplayMode", typeof(UIRectMode), typeof(UIRect), default(UIRectMode), BindingMode.TwoWay, validateValue: (bindable, value) => (UIRectMode)DisplayModeProperty.DefaultValue >= 0, propertyChanged:InvalidateDisplayMode);
  private UIRectMode _displayMode;
  public UIRectMode DisplayMode
  {
    get
    {
      return (UIRectMode)GetValue(DisplayModeProperty);
    }
    set
    {
      //if (value == _displayMode) return;
      SetValue(DisplayModeProperty, value);
      _displayMode = value;
      bool isVisible = true;
      short col = 2;
      switch (_displayMode)
      {
        case UIRectMode.None:
          lb2.Text = string.Empty;
          isVisible = false;
          col = 1;
          break;
        case UIRectMode.Default:
          lb2.Text = $"{_currentValue} / {_maxValue}";
          break;
        case UIRectMode.Value:
          lb2.Text = $"{_currentValue}";
          break;
        case UIRectMode.Percentage:
          lb2.Text = $"{_currentValue} / {_maxValue} ({((float)_currentValue / (float)_maxValue) * 100:0.00}%)";
          break;
      }
      lb2.IsVisible = isVisible;
      Grid.SetColumn(outerLine, col);
    }
  }

  /// <summary>Bindable property for <see cref="CurrentValue"/>.</summary>
  public static readonly BindableProperty CurrentValueProperty =
    BindableProperty.Create("CurrentValue", typeof(int), typeof(UIRect), default(int), BindingMode.TwoWay, validateValue: (bindable, value) => (int)CurrentValueProperty.DefaultValue >= 0, propertyChanged: InvalidateCurrentValue);
  private int _currentValue;
  public int CurrentValue
  {
    get
    {
      return (int)GetValue(CurrentValueProperty);
    }
    set
    {
      if (value > _maxValue) return;
      SetValue(CurrentValueProperty, value);
      _currentValue = value;
      switch (_displayMode)
      {
        case UIRectMode.Default:
          lb2.Text = $"{_currentValue} / {_maxValue}";
          break;
        case UIRectMode.Value:
          lb2.Text = $"{_currentValue}";
          break;
        case UIRectMode.Percentage:
          lb2.Text = $"{_currentValue} / {_maxValue} ({((float)_currentValue / (float)_maxValue) * 100:0.00}%)";
          break;
      }
      rect.ScaleX = ((float)_currentValue / (float)_maxValue);
    }
  }

  /// <summary>Bindable property for <see cref="MaxValueProperty"/>.</summary>
  public static readonly BindableProperty MaxValueProperty =
  BindableProperty.Create("MaxValue", typeof(int), typeof(UIRect), default(int), BindingMode.TwoWay, validateValue: (bindable, value) => (int)CurrentValueProperty.DefaultValue >= 0, propertyChanged: InvalidateMaxValue);
  private int _maxValue;
  public int MaxValue
  {
    get
    {
      return (int)GetValue(MaxValueProperty);
    }
    set
    {
      string str = value.ToString();
      if (str.Length > 6) return;
      SetValue(MaxValueProperty, value);
      _maxValue = value;
    }
  }

  static void InvalidateDisplayMode(BindableObject bindable, object oldValue, object newValue)
  {
    if (newValue is UIRectMode)
    {
      ((UIRect)bindable).DisplayMode = (UIRectMode)newValue;
    }
  }
  static void InvalidateCurrentValue(BindableObject bindable, object oldValue, object newValue)
  {
    if (bindable is UIRect grid)
    {
      ((UIRect)bindable).CurrentValue = (int)newValue;
    }
    else if (bindable is Element element && element.Parent is Grid parentGrid)
    {
      parentGrid.InvalidateMeasure();
    }
  }
  static void InvalidateMaxValue(BindableObject bindable, object oldValue, object newValue)
  {
    if (bindable is UIRect grid)
    {
      ((UIRect)bindable).MaxValue = (int)newValue;
      //((UIRect)bindable).lb2.Text = $"{((UIRect)bindable).CurrentValue} / {((UIRect)bindable).MaxValue}";
      //((UIRect)bindable).rect.ScaleX = ((float)((UIRect)bindable).CurrentValue / (float)((UIRect)bindable).MaxValue);
    }
    else if (bindable is Element element && element.Parent is Grid parentGrid)
    {
      parentGrid.InvalidateMeasure();
    }
  }

  /// <summary>Invalidates the measure of the view.</summary>
  protected override void InvalidateMeasure()
  {
    base.InvalidateMeasure();
    (this as IView)?.InvalidateMeasure();
  }

  public Brush? Color1
  {
    get
    {
      return outerLine.Stroke;
    }
    set
    {
      outerLine.Stroke = value;
    }
  }
  private Color _color2;
  public Color Color2
  {
    get
    {
      return _color2;
    }
    set
    {
      _color2 = value;
      lb1.TextColor = _color2;
      lb2.TextColor = _color2;
      rect.Background = _color2;
    }
  }
  public double FontSize1
  {
    get
    {
      return lb1.FontSize;
    }
    set
    {
      lb1.FontSize = value;
    }
  }
  public double FontSize2
  {
    get
    {
      return lb2.FontSize;
    }
    set
    {
      lb2.FontSize = value;
    }
  }
  public string Text
  {
    get
    {
      return lb1.Text;
    }
    set
    {
      lb1.Text = value;
    }
  }


  public UIRect()
  {
    InitializeComponent();
    DisplayMode = UIRectMode.Default;
  }
}

using Microsoft.Maui.Layouts;
using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.CControls;

public partial class UIStats : Grid
{
  private const string iBtnCloseGlyph = "\uE738";
  private const string iBtnOpenGlyph = "\uE710";
  private ObservableCollection<StatsModel> listStats { get; set; }

  private GroupBox gBoxSkills, gBoxTitles, gBoxDescriptions;
  private BoxView oldBoxVSelected;
  private Color[] stateTopColors;
  private int indexOldTopStats;

  public UIStats()
  {
    listStats = new();
    stateTopColors = [Colors.Transparent, Color.FromArgb("824402")];

    InitializeComponent();

    StatsModel playerStats = new()
    {
      HitPoints = 90,
      HitPointsMax = 100,
      MagicPoints = 100,
      MagicPointsMax = 180,
      StaminaPoints = 120,
      StaminaPointsMax = 200,
      EnergyPoints = 70,
      EnergyPointsMax = 100,
      Offense = 80,
      Defense = 90,
      Resistance = 240,
      MagicPower = 250,
      Speed = 200,
      SkillPoints = 100
    };
    listStats.Add(playerStats);
    listStats.Add(new StatsModel());

    Init();
  }

  private void Init()
  {
    BindableLayout.SetItemsSource(_stlStats, listStats);

    Grid grid = (Grid)((Border)_stlStats.Children[0]).Content;
    oldBoxVSelected = (BoxView)grid.Children[0];
    oldBoxVSelected.BackgroundColor = stateTopColors[1];

#if WINDOWS
    gBoxSkills = new GroupBox() { Title = "Skills", BackgroundColorTitle = Color.FromArgb("#2C2C2C"), Margin = new Thickness(4, 2, 2, 0) };
    Grid.SetRow(gBoxSkills, 5);
    Grid.SetColumnSpan(gBoxSkills, 2);
    Children.Add(gBoxSkills);

    gBoxTitles = new GroupBox() { Title = "Titles", BackgroundColorTitle = Color.FromArgb("#2C2C2C"), Margin = new Thickness(4, 2, 2, 0) };
    Grid.SetRow(gBoxTitles, 6);
    Grid.SetColumnSpan(gBoxTitles, 2);
    Children.Add(gBoxTitles);

    gBoxDescriptions = new GroupBox() { Title = "Descriptions", BackgroundColorTitle = Color.FromArgb("#2C2C2C"), Margin = new Thickness(4, 2, 2, 0) };
    Grid.SetRow(gBoxDescriptions, 7);
    Grid.SetColumnSpan(gBoxDescriptions, 2);
    Children.Add(gBoxDescriptions);
#elif ANDROID    
    FlexLayout items = new FlexLayout() { AlignContent = FlexAlignContent.Start, AlignItems = FlexAlignItems.Start, JustifyContent = FlexJustify.Start, Wrap = FlexWrap.Wrap, IsClippedToBounds = true };
    ScrollView scrollView = new ScrollView() { Orientation = ScrollOrientation.Vertical, Margin = new Thickness(10, 20, 5, 8) };
    scrollView.Content = items;

    Label[] title = new Label[3];
    title[0] = new Label() { Text = "Skills", Margin = new Thickness(7, 2, 1, 0), Padding = new Thickness(2, 0), HorizontalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start, VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start, LineBreakMode = LineBreakMode.HeadTruncation, BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("#2C2C2C"), FontSize = 12 };
    title[1] = new Label() { Text = "Titles", Margin = new Thickness(7, 2, 1, 0), Padding = new Thickness(2, 0), HorizontalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start, VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start, LineBreakMode = LineBreakMode.HeadTruncation, BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("#2C2C2C"), FontSize = 12 };
    title[2] = new Label() { Text = "Descriptions", Margin = new Thickness(7, 2, 1, 0), Padding = new Thickness(2, 0), HorizontalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start, VerticalOptions = LayoutOptions.Start, LineBreakMode = LineBreakMode.HeadTruncation, BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("#2C2C2C"), FontSize = 12 };
    StackLayout titles = new StackLayout() { Orientation = StackOrientation.Horizontal, Children = { title[0], title[1], title[2] } };

    Border border = new Border() { Stroke = Brush.White, StrokeThickness = 1, Margin = new Thickness(0, 10, 0, 0) };
    Grid gridGroupBox = new Grid();
    gridGroupBox.Children.Add(border);
    gridGroupBox.Children.Add(titles);
    gridGroupBox.Children.Add(scrollView);

    Grid.SetRow(gridGroupBox, 9);
    Grid.SetColumnSpan(gridGroupBox, 2);

    Children.Add(gridGroupBox);


#endif

    this.Loaded += UIStats_Loaded;
  }

  private void UIStats_Loaded(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    Grid grid = (Grid)((Border)_stlStats[0]).Content;
    ((View)grid[1]).IsVisible = false;
    ((View)grid[3]).IsVisible = false;
    grid.SetColumn(grid[0], 0);
    grid.SetColumnSpan(grid[0], 3);

    UpdateStats(listStats[0], 0);

    ((View)_stlStats[1]).IsVisible = false;
  }
  private void BtnStatsTop_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    IElement btn = (IElement)sender;
    byte index = (byte)_stlStats.Children.IndexOf((IView)btn.Parent.Parent);
    UpdateStats(listStats[index], index);
  }
  private void IBtnExpanderStats_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    if (listStats.Count == 1) return;
    ImageButton btn = (ImageButton)sender;
    FontImageSource font = (FontImageSource)btn.Source;
    if (font.Glyph == iBtnOpenGlyph) // Open
    {
      // Close it
      Grid grid = (Grid)btn.Parent;
      ExpanedStats(grid, font, false);
    }
    else // Close
    {
      // Open it
      Grid grid = (Grid)btn.Parent;
      ExpanedStats(grid, font, true);
    }
  }
  private void IBtnCloseStats_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    IElement btn = (IElement)sender;
    int index = _stlStats.Children.IndexOf((IView)btn.Parent.Parent);
    if (index == 0) return;
    //listStats.RemoveAt(index);
    ((Border)_stlStats[index]).IsVisible = false;
    UpdateStats(listStats[0], 0);

    //if (listStats.Count < 3)
    //{
    //  RowDefinitions[0].Height = GridLength.Auto;
    //}

    //if (listStats.Count == 1)
    //{
    //  Grid grid = (Grid)((Border)_stlStats.Children[0]).Content;
    //  ImageButton ibtn = (ImageButton)grid.Children[1];
    //  FontImageSource font = (FontImageSource)ibtn.Source;
    //  if (font.Glyph == iBtnCloseGlyph) // Close
    //  {
    //    // Open it
    //    ExpanedStats(grid, font, true);
    //  }
    //}
  }
  private void ExpanedStats(Grid grid, FontImageSource font, bool expand)
  {
    font.Glyph = expand ? iBtnOpenGlyph : iBtnCloseGlyph;
    grid.RowDefinitions[1].Height = new GridLength(expand ? 18 : 0);
    grid.RowDefinitions[2].Height = new GridLength(expand ? 18 : 0);
    grid.RowDefinitions[3].Height = new GridLength(expand ? 18 : 0);
    grid.RowDefinitions[4].Height = new GridLength(expand ? 18 : 0);
  }
  public void AddStats(StatsModel stats, bool expand = true)
  {
    if (listStats.Count > 5 || listStats.Any((x) => { return stats.Id == x.Id; }))
    {
      listStats[listStats.Count - 1] = stats;
      return;
    }
    else if (listStats.Count > 3)
    {
      RowDefinitions[0].Height = GridLength.Star;
    }
    else
    {
      listStats.Add(stats);
    }
    UpdateStats(stats, 1);
  }
  public void UpdateStats(StatsModel stats, byte index)
  {
    listStats[index] = stats;
    Grid grid = (Grid)((Border)_stlStats[index]).Content;
    if (index > 0)
    {
      ((Border)_stlStats[index]).IsVisible = true;
      ((View)grid[1]).IsVisible = true;
      ((View)grid[3]).IsVisible = true;
      grid.SetColumn(grid[0], 1);
      grid.SetColumnSpan(grid[0], 1);
    }
    UIRect uiRect = (UIRect)grid[4];
    uiRect.MaxValue = stats.HitPointsMax;
    uiRect.CurrentValue = stats.HitPoints;
    uiRect = (UIRect)grid[5];
    uiRect.MaxValue = stats.MagicPointsMax;
    uiRect.CurrentValue = stats.MagicPoints;
    uiRect = (UIRect)grid[6];
    uiRect.MaxValue = stats.StaminaPointsMax;
    uiRect.CurrentValue = stats.StaminaPoints;
    uiRect = (UIRect)grid[7];
    uiRect.MaxValue = stats.EnergyPointsMax;
    uiRect.CurrentValue = stats.EnergyPoints;

    _lbOffence.Text = stats.Offense.ToString();
    _lbDefence.Text = stats.Defense.ToString();
    _lbMagicPower.Text = stats.MagicPower.ToString();
    _lbResistance.Text = stats.Resistance.ToString();
    _lbSpeed.Text = stats.Speed.ToString();
    _lbSkillPoints.Text = stats.SkillPoints.ToString();

    SelectStats(grid[2]);
  }
  private void SelectStats(IElement sender)
  {
    int index = _stlStats.Children.IndexOf((IView)sender.Parent.Parent);
    if (index == indexOldTopStats) return;
    if (oldBoxVSelected != null)
    {
      oldBoxVSelected.BackgroundColor = stateTopColors[0];
    }
    indexOldTopStats = index;
    BoxView rect = (BoxView)((Grid)sender.Parent).Children[0];
    oldBoxVSelected = rect;
    rect.BackgroundColor = stateTopColors[1];
  }
}

using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Game
{
  public class WorldMap
  {
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public string Rank { get; set; }
    public ObservableCollection<MapPartModel> Templates { get; set; }
    public List<IMapPart[]> World { get; set; } = new();
    //public List<byte[]> ColumnsRows { get; set; } = new();

    private EmptyMapPartModel emptyPart = new();
    private char separate;

    public WorldMap()
    {
      separate = (char)15;
      //nullChar = (char)17;
      emptyPart.ColumnRows = new();
      emptyPart.Indices = new();
      Templates = new();

    }

    public void LoadMap()
    {
      List<object[]> dataObjects = Activities.Instance!.ReadData("WorldsMap", "Id,Rank,Templates", "0");
      int id = (int)dataObjects[0][0];
      Rank = ((byte[])dataObjects[0][1])[0].ToString();
      byte[] dataTemplates = (byte[])dataObjects[0][2];

      int index = -1;
      byte[] temp = new byte[4];
      byte len = dataTemplates[++index];
      // Load templates
      for (byte i = 0; i < len; i++)
      {
        Templates.Add(new MapPartModel());

        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
          temp[j] = dataTemplates[++index];
        Templates[i].Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0)));

        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
          temp[j] = dataTemplates[++index];
        Templates[i].BackgroundColor = Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0));

        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
          temp[j] = dataTemplates[++index];
        Templates[i].TextColor = Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0));

        Templates[i].Text = (i + 1).ToString();
      }

      World.Clear();
      ReadMap(id);

      int child = -1;
      for (int i = 0; i < World[0].Length; i++)
      {
        child = World[0][i].Child;
        if(child != -1)
          ReadMap(child);
      }
    }
    private void ReadMap(int id)
    {
      byte[] dataBytes = (byte[])Activities.Instance.ReadData("Map_00", "Data", $"{id}")[0][0];
      int index = -1;
      byte[] temp = new byte[4];
      byte len = dataBytes[++index];
      // Load the emptyMapPart-Colors
      if (len > 0)
      {
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
          temp[i] = dataBytes[++index];
        emptyPart.Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0)));

        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
          temp[i] = dataBytes[++index];
        emptyPart.BackgroundColor = Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0));

        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
          temp[i] = dataBytes[++index];
        emptyPart.TextColor = Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0));
      }

      MapPartModel[] emptyParts = new MapPartModel[len];
      emptyPart.Indices.Clear();

      // Load emptyMapParts
      for (byte i = 0; i < len; i++)
      {
        emptyParts[i] = new MapPartModel();
        emptyParts[i].PartType = MapPartType.Empty;
        emptyParts[i].Parent = -1;
        emptyParts[i].Child = -1;
        emptyParts[i].Text = "";
        emptyParts[i].Description = "";
        emptyParts[i].ColumnRow = new[]
        {
            dataBytes[++index],
            dataBytes[++index]
        };
        emptyParts[i].Stroke = emptyPart.Stroke;
        emptyParts[i].BackgroundColor = emptyPart.BackgroundColor;
        emptyParts[i].TextColor = emptyPart.TextColor;
        emptyParts[i].IsEnable = true;
        emptyParts[i].IsVisible = true;

        emptyPart.Indices.Add(dataBytes[++index]);
      }

      // Load the MapParts
      //_ = dataBytes[++index];
      MapPartModel[] mapParts = new MapPartModel[55];
      byte indexEmptyParts = 0;
      byte indexMapParts = 0;
      while (indexMapParts < mapParts.Length)
      {
        bool flag = indexMapParts == emptyPart.Indices[indexEmptyParts];
        if (flag)
        {
          mapParts[indexMapParts] = emptyParts[indexEmptyParts];
          indexEmptyParts += 1;
        }
        else
        {
          MapPartType mapType = (MapPartType)dataBytes[++index];
          switch (mapType)
          {
            case MapPartType.Player:
              mapParts[indexMapParts] = new MapPartModel();
              mapParts[indexMapParts].PartType = MapPartType.Player;
              break;
            case MapPartType.Other:
              mapParts[indexMapParts] = new MapPartModel();
              mapParts[indexMapParts].PartType = MapPartType.Other;
              break;
            case MapPartType.NPC:
              mapParts[indexMapParts] = new MapPartModel();
              mapParts[indexMapParts].PartType = MapPartType.NPC;
              break;
            case MapPartType.Item:
              mapParts[indexMapParts] = new MapPartModel();
              mapParts[indexMapParts].PartType = MapPartType.Item;
              break;
            case MapPartType.Object:
              mapParts[indexMapParts] = new MapPartModel();
              mapParts[indexMapParts].PartType = MapPartType.Object;
              break;
            case MapPartType.Map:
              mapParts[indexMapParts] = new MapPartModel();
              mapParts[indexMapParts].PartType = MapPartType.Map;
              break;
            default:
              mapParts[indexMapParts] = new MapPartModel();
              mapParts[indexMapParts].PartType = MapPartType.Empty;
              break;
          }
          index = mapParts[indexMapParts].LoadData(dataBytes, index);
        }
        indexMapParts += 1;
      }
      World.Add(mapParts);
    }

    public void SaveAll()
    {
      List<byte> dataBytes = new();
      byte[] temp;

      // Write the templates
      dataBytes.AddRange((byte)Templates.Count);
      for (byte i = 0; i < Templates.Count; i++)
      {
        // Stroke
        temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(((SolidColorBrush)Templates[i].Stroke).Color.ToUint());
        dataBytes.AddRange(temp);

        // BackgroundColor
        temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Templates[i].BackgroundColor.ToUint());
        dataBytes.AddRange(temp);

        // TextColor
        temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Templates[i].TextColor.ToUint());
        dataBytes.AddRange(temp);
      }
      Activities.Instance.InsertData("WorldsMap", "Id,Rank,Templates", "@Id,@Rank,@Templates", [0, (byte)0, (object)dataBytes.ToArray()]);

      // Write the world
      for (short i = 0; i < World.Count; i++)
      {
        dataBytes.Clear();
        List<IMapPart> parts = NormaliseParts(World[i]);
        dataBytes.Add((byte)emptyPart.ColumnRows.Count);
        if (emptyPart.ColumnRows.Count > 0)
        {
          // Stroke
          temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(((SolidColorBrush)emptyPart.Stroke).Color.ToUint());
          dataBytes.AddRange(temp);

          // BackgroundColor
          temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(emptyPart.BackgroundColor.ToUint());
          dataBytes.AddRange(temp);

          // TextColor
          temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(emptyPart.TextColor.ToUint());
          dataBytes.AddRange(temp);
        }
        for (byte j = 0; j < emptyPart.ColumnRows.Count; j++)
        {
          dataBytes.Add(emptyPart.ColumnRows[j][0]);
          dataBytes.Add(emptyPart.ColumnRows[j][1]);
          dataBytes.Add(emptyPart.Indices[j]);
        }
        for (byte j = 0; j < parts.Count; j++)
        {
          dataBytes.AddRange(parts[j].GetData());
        }

        //Activities.Instance.CreateTable("Map_00");
        Activities.Instance.InsertData("Map_00", "Id,Data", "@Id,@Data", [i, dataBytes.ToArray()]);
      }
    }

    private void ReadPart(BinaryReader reader, IMapPart part)
    {
      part.Parent = reader.ReadInt32();
      part.Child = reader.ReadInt32();

      if (reader.ReadBoolean())
        part.Text = null;
      else
        part.Text = reader.ReadString();
      if (reader.ReadBoolean())
        part.Description = null;
      else
        part.Description = reader.ReadString();

      part.ColumnRow = [reader.ReadByte(), reader.ReadByte()];
      part.Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromArgb(reader.ReadString()));
      part.BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb(reader.ReadString());
      part.TextColor = Color.FromArgb(reader.ReadString());
      part.IsEnable = reader.ReadBoolean();
      part.IsVisible = reader.ReadBoolean();
    }
    private void WritePart(BinaryWriter writer, IMapPart part)
    {
      writer.Write(part.Parent);
      writer.Write(part.Child);
      if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(part.Text))
      {
        writer.Write(true);
      }
      else
      {
        writer.Write(false);
        writer.Write(part.Text);
      }
      if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(part.Description))
      {
        writer.Write(true);
      }
      else
      {
        writer.Write(false);
        writer.Write(part.Description);
      }
      writer.Write(part.ColumnRow[0]);
      writer.Write(part.ColumnRow[1]);
      writer.Write(((SolidColorBrush)part.Stroke).Color.ToHex());
      writer.Write(part.BackgroundColor.ToHex());
      writer.Write(part.TextColor.ToHex());
      //writer.Write(World[i][j].Image.FileName);
      //writer.Write(World[i][j].Image.Image);
      writer.Write(part.IsEnable);
      writer.Write(part.IsVisible);
    }
    private List<IMapPart> NormaliseParts(IMapPart[] parts)
    {
      List<IMapPart> result = new();
      emptyPart.ColumnRows.Clear();
      emptyPart.Indices.Clear();
      byte index = 0;
      parts.ToList().ForEach(x =>
      {
        if (x.PartType == MapPartType.Empty)
        {
          emptyPart.Indices.Add(index);
          emptyPart.ColumnRows.Add(x.ColumnRow);
          emptyPart.Stroke = x.Stroke;
          emptyPart.BackgroundColor = x.BackgroundColor;
          emptyPart.TextColor = x.TextColor;
        }
        else
        {
          result.Add(x);
        }
        index++;
      });
      return result;
    }

using Microsoft.Data.SqlClient;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using MySql.Data.MySqlClient;
using System.Data;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;
using System.Web;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Game;

public sealed class Activities
{
  public static Activities? Instance { get; private set; }
  public static void Initialize()
  {
    Instance = new Activities();
  }

  private SqlConnection sqlConnection;
  private SqlCommand sqlCommand;
  private SqlDataReader sqlDataReader;

  private Activities()
  {
    //antasyworlds
    //String deviceToken = HttpUtility.UrlEncode("YourDeviceToken");
    //String passphrase = HttpUtility.UrlEncode("YourPassphrase");
    //Data Source=(LocalDB)\MSSQLLocalDB;AttachDbFilename=D:\Projects\MUI\ReGame\SharedCode\Game\DataFile.mdf;Integrated Security=True
    //ALTER DATABASE CURRENT
    //SET AUTO_SHRINK ON;
    //MODIFY FILE(NAME = 'DataFile', FILEGROWTH = 512MB);

    sqlConnection = new SqlConnection(@"Data Source=(LocalDB)\MSSQLLocalDB;AttachDbFilename=D:\Projects\MUI\ReGame\SharedCode\Game\DataFile.mdf;Integrated Security=True");
    sqlCommand = new SqlCommand();
    sqlCommand.Connection = sqlConnection;

  }
  public List<object[]> ReadData(string table, string columns, string ids)
  {
    string query = $"SELECT {columns} FROM {table} WHERE Id IN ({ids})";
    sqlCommand.CommandText = query;

    string[] cols = columns.Split(',');
    List<object[]> binaryData = new();

    sqlConnection.Open();
    sqlDataReader = sqlCommand.ExecuteReader();
    while (sqlDataReader.Read())
    {
      object[] dataColumns = new object[cols.Length];
      for (int i = 0; i < cols.Length; i++)
        dataColumns[i] = sqlDataReader[cols[i]];
      binaryData.Add(dataColumns);
    }

    sqlConnection.Close();

    return binaryData;
  }
  public void InsertData(string table, string columns, string columnNames, object[] values)
  {
    string query = $"INSERT INTO {table} ({columns}) VALUES ({columnNames})";
    sqlCommand.CommandText = query;
    string[] cols = columnNames.Split(',');
    sqlCommand.Parameters.Clear();
    for (int i = 0; i < cols.Length; i++)
    {
      sqlCommand.Parameters.AddWithValue($"{cols[i]}", values[i]);
    }
    sqlConnection.Open();
    sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
    sqlConnection.Close(); 
  }

  public void CreateTable(string nameTable)
  {
    string query = $@"CREATE TABLE {nameTable}
                      (
                          ID INT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY,
                          Data VARBINARY(MAX) NULL
                      );";
    sqlCommand.CommandText = query;
    sqlCommand.Parameters.Clear();

    sqlConnection.Open();
    sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
    sqlConnection.Close();
  }

  //SendPost("http://localhost/ReGame/", String.Format("deviceToken={0}&passphrase={1}", deviceToken, passphrase));
  //    SendData();
  private async Task SendData1()
  {
    using (HttpClient client = new HttpClient())
    {
      byte[] data = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes("Hello World");
      BitConverter.ToString(data, 0, data.Length);
      var content = new ByteArrayContent(data);
      content.Headers.ContentType = new System.Net.Http.Headers.MediaTypeHeaderValue("application/octet-stream");

      var response = await client.PostAsync("http://localhost/ReGame/", content);
      var responseString = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();

      Console.WriteLine(responseString);
    }
  }
  public string SendPost1(string url, string postData)
  {
    string webpageContent = string.Empty;

    try
    {
      byte[] byteArray = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(postData);

      HttpWebRequest webRequest = (HttpWebRequest)WebRequest.Create(url);
      webRequest.Method = "POST";
      webRequest.ContentType = "application/x-www-form-urlencoded";
      webRequest.ContentLength = byteArray.Length;

      using (Stream webpageStream = webRequest.GetRequestStream())
      {
        webpageStream.Write(byteArray, 0, byteArray.Length);
      }

      using (HttpWebResponse webResponse = (HttpWebResponse)webRequest.GetResponse())
      {
        using (StreamReader reader = new StreamReader(webResponse.GetResponseStream()))
        {
          webpageContent = reader.ReadToEnd();
        }
      }
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
      //throw or return an appropriate response/exception
    }

    return webpageContent;
  }
}

using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using Point = System.Drawing.Point;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Game;

public sealed class Profile
{
  public static Profile? Instance { get; private set; }
  public static void Initialize()
  {
    Instance = new Profile();
  }

  #region Users
  public string Name { get; private set; }
  public string Info { get; private set; }
  public short Level { get; private set; }
  public byte Age { get; private set; }
  public int AmountGold { get; private set; }
  public CharacterModel Character { get; private set; } = new();
  public Point Location { get; private set; } = new();
  public bool Tutorial { get; private set; } = false;
#endregion
  #region UsersStates
  public bool IsOnline { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInGame { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInBattle { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInShop { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInInventory { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInSettings { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInFriends { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInQuests { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInAchievements { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInProfile { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInChat { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInEvents { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInLeaderboard { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInGuild { get; private set; } = false;
  public bool IsInClan { get; private set; } = false;
  #endregion

  private float nextEpCounter = 1;
  private int experience, nextEp = 100;

  private Profile()
  {
    //Activities.Instance.InsertData("Users", "Character", "@Character", [Character.GetData()]);
  }

  public void LoadData()
  {


    List<object[]> dataObjects = Activities.Instance.ReadData("Users", "Name,Info,Level,Gold,Age,XP,Location,Character,Tutorial", "0");
    Name = dataObjects[0][0].ToString();
    Info = dataObjects[0][1].ToString();
    Level = (short)dataObjects[0][2];
    AmountGold = (int)dataObjects[0][3];
    Age = ((byte[])(dataObjects[0][4]))[0];
    experience = (int)dataObjects[0][5];
    Location = (Point)dataObjects[0][6];
    Character.LoadData((byte[])dataObjects[0][7], 0);
    Tutorial = (bool)dataObjects[0][8];

  }

  public void SetExperience(int experience)
  {
    experience = (int)(nextEpCounter * experience);
    this.experience += experience;
    if (experience >= nextEp)
    {
      this.experience = 0;
      nextEpCounter -= .001f;
      Level++;
    }
  }
}

using ReGame.SharedCode.Game.SystermItems;
using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Game.JobClasses;

public class MageClass : ClassModel
{
  public MageSkills Skills { get; set; } = new();

  public MageClass()
  {
    // Initialize the Skills property to avoid null
    Skills.MagicAOR((short)MageMagicsAll.Elementa, false);

  }

  public override void Active(int id, CharacterModel target)
  {
  }

  public override short GetSize()
  {
    short size = base.GetSize();
    size += Skills.GetSize();
    return size;
  }
  public override byte[] GetData()
  {
    short index = -1;
    byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];
    data.CopyTo(base.GetData(), ++index);
    data.CopyTo(Skills.GetData(), ++index);
    return data;
  }
  public override int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
  {
    index = base.LoadData(data, index);
    index = Skills.LoadData(data, index);
    return index;
  }
}

using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Game.SystermItems;

public enum MageFireSkills : byte
{
  /// <summary>
  /// A flaming projectile that explodes on impact.
  /// </summary>
  Fireball = 1,
  /// <summary>
  /// A massive fireball with devastating power.
  /// </summary>
  Pyroblast = 2,
  /// <summary>
  /// A fiery explosion that scorches a wide area.
  /// </summary>
  Flamestrike = 4,
  /// <summary>
  /// Ignites enemies for continuous burning damage.
  /// </summary>
  Combustion = 8,
}
public enum MageFrostSkills : byte
{
  /// <summary>

  Frostbolt = 1,
  /// <summary>

{
  /// <summary>
  /// Fire, water, wind, earth, lightning, ice, and even more exotic elements like darkness and light.
  /// </summary>

}

public class MageSkills : ISkills
{
  /// <summary>
  /// 1=Elemental,2=Summoning,4=Healing,8=Illusion,16=TimeManipulation,32=Space,64=Necromancy,128=Transformation,256=Gravity,512=Mind
  /// </summary>
  public short Skills { get; set; }

  /// <summary>
  /// Fire = 0, Frost = 1, Arcane = 2
  /// </summary>
  public byte SelectedSkillType { get; set; }
  public Dictionary<byte, SkillModel> ListSkills { get; set; } = new();

  public MageSkills()
  {
    
  }

  /// <summary>
  /// Add or Remove Magic to the <see cref="Skills"/>
  /// </summary>
  /// <param name="mageMagic">Magic element.</param>
  /// <param name="Remove">true for Remove, false for Add.</param>
  /// <returns>If it was success return true otherwise false.</returns>
  public bool MagicAOR(short mageMagic, bool Remove)
  {
    if (Remove && Skills - mageMagic < 1) return false;
    else if (!Remove && Skills + mageMagic > 1023) return false;
    Skills = Remove ? (short)(Skills - mageMagic) : (short)(Skills + mageMagic);
    return true;
  }
  public void MageMagic()
  {
    switch (Skills)
    {
      case 1:// 1
        break;
      case 3:// 1,2
        break;
      case 7:// 1,2,4
        break;
      case 15:// 1,2,4,8
        break;
      case 31:// 1,2,4,8,16
        break;
      case 63:// 1,2,4,8,16,32
        break;
      case 127:// 1,2,4,8,16,32,64
        break;
      case 255:// 1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128
        break;
      case 511:// 1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256
        break;
      case 1023:// 1,2,4,8,16,32,64,128,256,512
        break;
    }
  }

  /// <summary>
  /// Add new skill
  /// </summary>
  /// <param name="skill">Represent of which skill to add</param>
  /// <param name="skillType">Select between Fire = 0, Frost = 1, Arcane = 2</param>
  /// <returns>If it was success return true otherwise false.</returns>
  public bool AddSkill(byte skill, byte skillType)
  {
    byte idSkill = (byte)(skill * skillType);
    if (ListSkills.ContainsKey(idSkill)) return false;
    SkillModel skillModel = new SkillModel()
    {
      Level = 1,
    };
    ListSkills.Add(idSkill, skillModel);
    SelectedSkillType = skillType;
    return true;
  }

  public short GetSize()
  {
    short size = 5;
    KeyValuePair<byte,SkillModel> keyValue;
    for (int i = 0; i < ListSkills.Count; i++)
    {
      keyValue = ListSkills.ElementAt(i);
      size += 1;
      size += keyValue.Value.GetSize();
    }
    return size;
  }
  public byte[] GetData()
  {
    short index = -1;
    byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];

    byte[] temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Skills);
    for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
      data[++index] = temp[i];

    data[++index] = SelectedSkillType;

    KeyValuePair<byte, SkillModel> keyValue;
    temp = BitConverter.GetBytes((short)ListSkills.Count);
    for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
      data[++index] = temp[i];
    for (int i = 0; i < ListSkills.Count; i++)
    {
      keyValue = ListSkills.ElementAt(i);
      data[++index] = keyValue.Key;
      temp = keyValue.Value.GetData();
      data.CopyTo(temp, ++index);
    }
    return data;
  }
  public int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
  {
    byte[] temp = new byte[2]; // short
    for (byte i = 0; i < 2; i++)
      temp[i] = data[++index];
    Skills = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);

    SelectedSkillType = data[++index];

    temp = new byte[2]; // short
    for (byte i = 0; i < 2; i++)
      temp[i] = data[++index];
    short count = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);
    ListSkills = new();
    for (short i = 0; i < count; i++)
    {
      byte key = data[++index];
      SkillModel value = new();
      value.LoadData(data, index);
      ListSkills.Add(key, value);
    }
    return index;
  }
}

  public class StatsModel : IBinaryData
  {
    private static int CurrentId
    {
      get;
      set;
    } = -1;
    public int Id
    {
      get;
      private set;
    }
    public int HitPoints { get; set; }
    public int HitPointsMax { get; set; }

    public int MagicPoints { get; set; }
    public int MagicPointsMax { get; set; }

    public int StaminaPoints { get; set; }
    public int StaminaPointsMax { get; set; }

    public int EnergyPoints { get; set; }
    public int EnergyPointsMax { get; set; }

    public int Offense { get; set; }
    public int Defense { get; set; }
    public int Resistance { get; set; }
    public int MagicPower { get; set; }
    public int Speed { get; set; }
    public int SkillPoints { get; set; }

    public StatsModel()
    {
      Id = ++CurrentId;
    }

    public short GetSize()
    {
      return 15 * 4;
    }
    public byte[] GetData()
    {
      short index = -1;
      byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];

      byte[] temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Id);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(HitPoints);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(HitPointsMax);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(MagicPoints);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(MagicPointsMax);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(StaminaPoints);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(StaminaPointsMax);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(EnergyPoints);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(EnergyPointsMax);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Offense);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Defense);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Resistance);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(MagicPower);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Speed);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(SkillPoints);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      return data;
    }
    public int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
    {
      byte[] temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      CurrentId = Id = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      HitPoints = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      HitPointsMax = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      MagicPoints = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      MagicPointsMax = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      StaminaPoints = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      StaminaPointsMax = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      EnergyPoints = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      EnergyPointsMax = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Offense = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Defense = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Resistance = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      MagicPower = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Speed = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      SkillPoints = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);

      return index;
    }
  }

  public class RacesInfoModel
  {
    public string? HumansInfo =>
      "Humans\r\n" +
      "The most widespread and adaptable race in the known world.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Humans are defined not by any single strength, but by their extraordinary capacity to learn, evolve, and persevere. " +
      "Where other races excel in one domain, Humans thrive across all of them — making them the most unpredictable and dangerous opponents, and the most reliable allies.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Stat Affinities: Balanced across all attributes with no hard weaknesses.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Strengths: Fast learners, highly versatile, excellent at combining skill sets from different disciplines.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Weaknesses: No natural elemental resistances, no innate magical abilities, shorter lifespan limits accumulated wisdom.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Lore: Human civilizations rise and fall at a pace that astonishes elder races. In a single generation, they can rebuild cities, forge empires, or spark revolutions. " +
      "Their ambition is both their greatest virtue and their most dangerous flaw.";

      "Their patience is legendary — and so is their reluctance to act hastily.\r\n\r\n" +

      "Weaknesses: Physically fragile, slow to reproduce, can be emotionally detached or arrogant toward younger races.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Lore: Elven society is divided into distinct cultures — wood elves who commune with ancient forests, high elves who pursue arcane perfection in towering spires, " +
      "and dark elves who dwell in the deep places of the world, their magic twisted by centuries of isolation. Despite their differences, all elves share an unshakeable bond with the flow of magical energy.";

      "Strengths: Complete command over plant growth and manipulation, passive healing sustained by proximity to nature, ability to merge with and travel through trees, communication network with other forest spirits across vast distances.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Weaknesses: Bond to their source tree means its destruction results in the Dryad's death or severe diminishment, fire is their most feared adversary, power drops dramatically outside of forested environments.\r\n\r\n" +
      "Lore: Ancient Dryads whose trees have stood for thousands of years are considered among the wisest beings in existence. They remember the world before roads were cut and cities were built, " +
      "and they hold that knowledge with a profound sadness that makes them both melancholy and deeply purposeful in their defense of what remains.";

  /// <summary>
  /// This info must not exist in the source code and must be loaded from the server.
  /// </summary>
  public enum RacesEnum
  {
    Humans,
    Elves,
    Dwarves,
    Orcs,
    BeastKin,
    Undead,
    Demons,
    Angels,
    Halflings,
    Goblins,
    Trolls,
    Giants,
    Dragonoids,
    Fae,
    Nymphs,
    Merfolk,
    Vampires,
    Werewolves,
    Kitsune,
    Oni,
    Dryads,
    Golems,
    Elementals,
    DemiGods,
    Celestials,
    Fairies,

    ShadowKin,
    Starborn,
    Voidborn,
    Phoenix,

    Valkyries,
    Norns,
    Titans
  }

  public class RaceModel : IBinaryData
  {
    public string? Name { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Description { get; set; } = "";

    public short GetSize()
    {
      short size = 0;
      if (Name != null) Name = "";
      size += (short)(Name.Length + 1); // string = 1 + 1
      if (Description != null) Description = "";
      size += (short)(Description.Length + 2); // string = 2 + 1
      return size;
    }
    public byte[] GetData()
    {
      short index = -1;
      byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];

      // Name
      byte lenText = Name.Length <= byte.MaxValue ? (byte)Name.Length : byte.MaxValue;
      data[++index] = lenText;
      for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
        data[++index] = (byte)Name[i];

      // Description
      short lenDes = Description.Length <= short.MaxValue ? (short)Description.Length : short.MaxValue;
      byte[] temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(lenDes);
      data[++index] = temp[0];
      data[++index] = temp[1];
      for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
        data[++index] = (byte)Description[i];

      return data;
    }
    public int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
    {
      byte lenText = data[++index];// byte
      for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
        Name += (char)data[++index];

      byte[] temp = new byte[2]; // short
      temp[0] = data[++index];
      temp[1] = data[++index];
      short lenDes = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);
      for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
        Description += (char)data[++index];

      return index;
    }
  }
  public class InventoryModel : IBinaryData
  {
    public int Amount { get; set; }
    public int[]? Items { get; set; } = [];

    public short GetSize()
    {
      return (short)(4 + Items.Length * 5);
    }
    public byte[] GetData()
    {
      short index = -1;
      byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];

      byte[] temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Amount);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Items.Length);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      for (int i = 0; i < Items.Length; i++)
      {
        temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Items[i]);
        for (int j = 0; j < temp.Length; j++)
          data[++index] = temp[j];
      }
      return data;
    }
    public int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
    {
      byte[] temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Amount = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);

      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Items = new int[BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0)];

      for (int i = 0; i < Items.Length; i++)
      {
        temp = new byte[4]; // int
        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
          temp[j] = data[++index];
        Items[i] = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);
      }
      return index;
    }
  }
  public class CharacterModel : MapPartModel, ICloneable
  {
    public RaceModel? Race { get; set; }
    public StatsModel? Stats { get; set; }
    public InventoryModel? Inventory { get; set; }
    public EquipsModel? Equips { get; set; }
    public IClass? CharacterClass { get; set; }

    public CharacterModel() { }
    public CharacterModel(IMapPart mapPart)
    {
      LoadMapPart(mapPart);
    }

    public new object Clone()
    {
      return new CharacterModel
      {
        PartType = this.PartType,
        Parent = this.Parent,
        Child = this.Child,
        Text = this.Text,
        Description = this.Description,
        ColumnRow = this.ColumnRow,
        Stroke = this.Stroke,
        BackgroundColor = this.BackgroundColor,
        Image = this.Image,
        TextColor = this.TextColor,
        IsEnable = this.IsEnable,
        IsVisible = this.IsVisible,
        Race = this.Race,
        Stats = this.Stats,
        Inventory = this.Inventory,
        Equips = this.Equips,
        CharacterClass = this.CharacterClass,
      };
    }

    public new short GetSize()
    {
      short size = base.GetSize();
      size += Race.GetSize();
      size += Stats.GetSize();
      size += Inventory.GetSize();
      size += Equips.GetSize();
      size += CharacterClass.GetSize();
      return size;
    }
    public new byte[] GetData()
    {
      short index = -1;
      byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];
      data.CopyTo(base.GetData(), ++index);
      data.CopyTo(Race.GetData(), ++index);
      data.CopyTo(Stats.GetData(), ++index);
      data.CopyTo(Inventory.GetData(), ++index);
      data.CopyTo(Equips.GetData(), ++index);
      data.CopyTo(CharacterClass.GetData(), ++index);
      return data;
    }
    public new int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
    {
      index = base.LoadData(data, index);
      index = Race.LoadData(data, index);
      index = Stats.LoadData(data, index);
      index = Inventory.LoadData(data, index);
      index = Equips.LoadData(data, index);
      index = CharacterClass.LoadData(data, index);
      return index;
    }
  }
}

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Models;

public class SkillModel : IBinaryData
{
  public byte Level { get; set; }
  public short XP { get; set; }
  public short Required { get; set; }
  public string Name { get; set; } = "";
  public string Description { get; set; } = "";

  public short GetSize()
  {
    short size = 5;
    if (Name == null) Name = "";
    size += (short)(Name.Length + 1); // string = 1 + 1
    if (Description == null) Description = "";
    size += (short)(Description.Length + 2); // string = 2 + 1
    return size;
  }
  public byte[] GetData()
  {
    short index = -1;
    byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];

    data[++index] = Level;

    byte[] temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(XP);
    for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
      data[++index] = temp[i];
    temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Required);
    for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
      data[++index] = temp[i];

    // Text
    byte lenText = Name.Length <= byte.MaxValue ? (byte)Name.Length : byte.MaxValue;
    data[++index] = lenText;
    for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
      data[++index] = (byte)Name[i];

    // Description
    short lenDes = Description.Length <= short.MaxValue ? (short)Description.Length : short.MaxValue;
    temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(lenDes);
    data[++index] = temp[0];
    data[++index] = temp[1];
    for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
      data[++index] = (byte)Description[i];

    return data;
  }
  public int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
  {
    Level = data[++index];

    byte[] temp = new byte[2]; // short
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
      temp[i] = data[++index];
    XP = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);

    temp = new byte[2]; // short
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
      temp[i] = data[++index];
    XP = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);

    byte lenText = data[++index];// byte
    for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
      Name += (char)data[++index];

    temp = new byte[2]; // short
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
      temp[i] = data[++index];
    short lenDes = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);
    for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
      Description += (char)data[++index];

    return index;
  }
}
public interface ISkills : IBinaryData
{
  public short Skills { get; }
  byte SelectedSkillType { get; }
  Dictionary<byte, SkillModel> ListSkills { get; }
}
public interface IClass : IBinaryData
{
  string? Name { get; }
  string? Description { get; }
  void Active(int id, CharacterModel target);
}
public class ClassModel : IClass
{
  public string? Name { get; set; }
  public string? Description { get; set; }

  public virtual void Active(int id, CharacterModel target) { }

  public virtual short GetSize()
  {
    short size = 0;
    if (Name == null) Name = "";
    size += (short)(Name.Length + 1); // string = 2 + 1
    if (Description == null) Description = "";
    Description += (short)(Description.Length + 1); // string = 2 + 1
    return size;
  }
  public virtual byte[] GetData()
  {
    short index = -1;
    byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];

    // Text
    byte lenText = Name.Length <= byte.MaxValue ? (byte)Name.Length : byte.MaxValue;
    data[++index] = lenText;
    for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
      data[++index] = (byte)Name[i];

    // Description
    short lenDes = Description.Length <= short.MaxValue ? (short)Description.Length : short.MaxValue;
    byte[] temp = temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(lenDes);
    data[++index] = temp[0];
    data[++index] = temp[1];
    for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
      data[++index] = (byte)Description[i];

    return data;
  }
  public virtual int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
  {
    byte lenText = data[++index];// byte
    for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
      Name += (char)data[++index];

    byte[] temp = new byte[2]; // short
    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
      temp[i] = data[++index];
    short lenDes = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);
    for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
      Description += (char)data[++index];

    return index;
  }
}

using System.Reflection;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Models;

/// <summary>
/// Represents of 32 bit-signed integer.
/// </summary>
public struct DefaultInt
{
  public int MaxValue { get; private set; }
  private int _Value;
  private string _Format = "";

  private DefaultInt(int value)
  {
    _Value = value;
  }

  public bool Equals(DefaultInt? obj)
  {
    return obj.Value == _Value;
  }
  public override bool Equals(object? obj)
  {
    return base.Equals(obj);
  }
  public override int GetHashCode()
  {
    return base.GetHashCode();
  }
  public string ToString(string fomat)
  {
    return String.Format(fomat, _Value);
  }
  public string ToString(bool showFormat)
  {
    return $"{(showFormat ? _Format + _Value : _Value)}";
  }
  public override string ToString()
  {
    return "" + _Value;
  }

  #region Operators Methods
  /// <summary>Adds two DefaultValues together.</summary>
  /// <param name="left">The first DefaultValue to add.</param>
  /// <param name="right">The second DefaultValue to add.</param>
  /// <returns>The summed DefaultValue.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="op_Addition" /> method defines the addition operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator +(DefaultInt left, DefaultInt right)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(left._Value + right._Value);
  }
  /// <summary>Negates the specified DefaultValue.</summary>
  /// <param name="value">The DefaultValue to negate.</param>
  /// <returns>The negated DefaultValue.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="op_UnaryNegation" /> method defines the unary negation operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator -(DefaultInt value)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(-value._Value);
  }
  /// <summary>Subtracts the second DefaultValue from the first.</summary>
  /// <param name="left">The first DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <param name="right">The second DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <returns>The DefaultValue that results from subtracting <paramref name="right" /> from <paramref name="left" />.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="op_Subtraction" /> method defines the subtraction operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator -(DefaultInt left, DefaultInt right)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(left._Value - right._Value);
  }
  /// <summary>Multiplies the scalar value by the specified DefaultValue.</summary>
  /// <param name="left">The DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <param name="right">The scalar value.</param>
  /// <returns>The scaled DefaultValue.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="DefaultInt.op_Multiply" /> method defines the multiplication operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator *(int left, DefaultInt right)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(left * right._Value);
  }
  /// <summary>Multiplies the specified DefaultValue by the specified scalar value.</summary>
  /// <param name="left">The DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <param name="right">The scalar value.</param>
  /// <returns>The scaled DefaultValue.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="DefaultInt.op_Multiply" /> method defines the multiplication operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator *(DefaultInt left, int right)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(left._Value * right);
  }
  /// <summary>Returns a new DefaultValue whose values are the product of each pair of elements in two specified DefaultValues.</summary>
  /// <param name="left">The first DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <param name="right">The second DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <returns>The element-wise product DefaultValue.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="DefaultInt.op_Multiply" /> method defines the multiplication operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator *(DefaultInt left, DefaultInt right)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(left._Value * right._Value);
  }
  /// <summary>Divides the first DefaultValue by the second.</summary>
  /// <param name="left">The first DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <param name="right">The second DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <returns>The DefaultValue that results from dividing <paramref name="left" /> by <paramref name="right" />.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="DefaultInt.op_Division" /> method defines the division operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator /(DefaultInt left, DefaultInt right)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(left._Value / right._Value);
  }
  /// <summary>Divides the specified DefaultValue by a specified scalar value.</summary>
  /// <param name="value1">The DefaultValue.</param>
  /// <param name="value2">The scalar value.</param>
  /// <returns>The result of the division.</returns>
  /// <remarks>The <see cref="DefaultInt.op_Division" /> method defines the division operation for <see cref="DefaultInt" /> objects.</remarks>
  public static DefaultInt operator /(DefaultInt value1, int value2)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(value1._Value / value2);
  }
  /// <summary>
  /// Returns a value that indicates whether each pair of elements in two specified DefaultValues is equal.
  /// </summary>
  /// <param name="left">The first DefaultValue to compare.</param>
  /// <param name="right"></param>
  /// <returns>true if left and right are equal; otherwise, false.</returns>
  public static bool operator ==(DefaultInt left, DefaultInt right)
  {
    return left._Value == right._Value;
  }
  /// <summary>
  /// Returns a value that indicates whether two specified DefaultValues are not equal.
  /// </summary>
  /// <param name="left">The first DefaultValue to compare.</param>
  /// <param name="right">The second DefaultValue to compare.</param>
  /// <returns>true if left and right are not equal; otherwise, false.</returns>
  public static bool operator !=(DefaultInt left, DefaultInt right)
  {
    return !(left._Value == right._Value);
  }
  public static implicit operator DefaultInt(int v)
  {
    return new DefaultInt(v);
  }
  public static implicit operator int(DefaultInt v)
  {
    return v._Value;
  }
  public static implicit operator string(DefaultInt v)
  {
    return "" + v._Value;
  }
  #endregion
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Models
{
  public class MapNavigationModel
  {
    public int Parent { get; set; }
    public byte Columns { get; set; }
    public byte Rows { get; set; }
    public string Text { get; set; }

    /// <summary>
    /// Get the actual size of the each property in bytes
    /// </summary>
    /// <returns> Return 0 if it is empty or null </returns>
    public long GetSize()
    {
      //Single = 4, float = 4, double = 8, long = 8, decimal = 16
      // bool = 1, char = 1, byte = 1
      // int = 4, uint = 4, short = 2, ushort = 2

      long size = 6; // 4 + 1 * 2
      if (Text != null)
        size += Text.Length + 1; // string = 2 + 1

      return size;
    }
  }
}

using Microsoft.Maui.Graphics.Converters;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Numerics;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Models
{
  [DebuggerDisplay("Column={Col}, Row={Row}")]
  [TypeConverter(typeof(PointTypeConverter))]
  public struct GridPoint
  {
    public static readonly GridPoint Zero = new(0,0);

    public byte Column { get; set; }

    public byte Row { get; set; }

    public bool IsEmpty => Column == 0 && Row == 0;

    public static implicit operator Size(GridPoint pos)
    {
      return new Size(pos.Column, pos.Row);
    }

    public static implicit operator Point(GridPoint pos)
    {
      return new Point(pos.Column, pos.Row);
    }

    public override string ToString()
    {
      return $"{{Column={Column.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)} Row={Row.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)}}}";
    }

    public GridPoint(byte col, byte row)
    {
      this = default(GridPoint);
      Column = col;
      Row = row;
    }

    public GridPoint(Size sz)
    {
      this = default(GridPoint);
      Column = (byte)sz.Width;
      Row = (byte)sz.Height;
    }

    public GridPoint(SizeF sz)
    {
      this = default(GridPoint);
      Column = (byte)sz.Width;
      Row = (byte)sz.Height;
    }

    public GridPoint(Vector2 v)
    {
      Column = (byte)v.X;
      Row = (byte)v.Y;
    }

    public override bool Equals(object o)
    {
      if (!(o is GridPoint))
      {
        return false;
      }

      return this == (GridPoint)o;
    }

    public bool Equals(object o, byte epsilon)
    {
      if (!(o is GridPoint pos))
      {
        return false;
      }

      if (Math.Abs(pos.Column - Column) < epsilon)
      {
        return Math.Abs(pos.Row - Row) < epsilon;
      }

      return false;
    }

    public override int GetHashCode()
    {
      return Column.GetHashCode() ^ (Row.GetHashCode() * 397);
    }

    public GridPoint Offset(byte posX, byte posY)
    {
      GridPoint result = this;
      result.Column += posX;
      result.Row += posY;
      return result;
    }

    public static explicit operator SizeF(GridPoint pos)
    {
      return new SizeF(pos.Column, pos.Row);
    }

    public static GridPoint operator +(GridPoint pos, SizeF sz)
    {
      return new GridPoint((byte)(pos.Column + sz.Width), (byte)(pos.Row + sz.Height));
    }

    public static Size operator -(GridPoint posA, GridPoint posB)
    {
      return new Size(posA.Column - posB.Column, posA.Row - posB.Row);
    }

    public static GridPoint operator -(GridPoint pos, SizeF sz)
    {
      return new GridPoint((byte)(pos.Column - sz.Width), (byte)(pos.Row - sz.Height));
    }

    public static bool operator ==(GridPoint posA, GridPoint posB)
    {
      if (posA.Column == posB.Column)
      {
        return posA.Row == posB.Row;
      }

      return false;
    }

    public static bool operator !=(GridPoint posA, GridPoint posB)
    {
      if (posA.Column == posB.Column)
      {
        return posA.Row != posB.Row;
      }

      return true;
    }

    public void Deconstruct(out byte col, out byte row)
    {
      col = Column;
      row = Row;
    }

    public static implicit operator PointF(GridPoint pos)
    {
      return new PointF(pos.Column, pos.Row);
    }

    public static implicit operator GridPoint(Vector2 v)
    {
      return new GridPoint(v);
    }

    public static bool TryParse(string value, out GridPoint point)
    {
      if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value))
      {
        string[] array = value.Split(',');
        if (array.Length == 2 && byte.TryParse(array[0], NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result) && byte.TryParse(array[1], NumberStyles.Number, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result2))
        {
          point = new GridPoint(result, result2);
          return true;
        }
      }

      point = default(GridPoint);
      return false;
    }
  }
  public class PartModel
  {
    public GridPoint ColumnRow { get; set; }
  }
  public class ImageObject
  {
    public string FileName { get; set; }
    public ImageSource Image { get; set; }
  }
  public interface IBinaryData
  {
    /// <summary>
    /// Get the sum of each variable
    /// </summary>
    /// <returns>The total sum of all variable</returns>
    short GetSize();
    /// <summary>
    /// Get the all data in a listof bytes
    /// </summary>
    /// <returns>Get all data in bytes</returns>
    byte[] GetData();
    /// <summary>
    /// Load all data in the class
    /// </summary>
    /// <param name="data">All data that have all of varialbes</param>
    /// <param name="index">Start loading at the index</param>
    /// <returns>The amount of data has been loaded</returns>
    int LoadData(byte[] data, int index);
  }
  public interface IMapPart : ICloneable, IBinaryData
  {
    MapPartType PartType { get; set; }
    int Parent { get; set; }
    int Child { get; set; }
    string? Text { get; set; }
    string? Description { get; set; }
    byte[]? ColumnRow { get; set; }
    Brush? Stroke { get; set; }
    Color? BackgroundColor { get; set; }
    ImageObject? Image { get; set; }
    Color? TextColor { get; set; }
    bool IsEnable { get; set; }
    bool IsVisible { get; set; }
  }
  public class MapPartModel : IMapPart
  {
    public MapPartType PartType { get; set; }
    public int Parent { get; set; }
    public int Child { get; set; }
    public string? Text { get; set; } = "";
    public string? Description { get; set; } = "";
    public byte[]? ColumnRow { get; set; }
    public Brush? Stroke { get; set; }
    public Color? BackgroundColor { get; set; }
    public ImageObject? Image { get; set; }
    public Color? TextColor { get; set; }
    public bool IsEnable { get; set; }
    public bool IsVisible { get; set; }

    public short GetSize()
    {
      //Single = 4, float = 4, double = 8, long = 8, decimal = 16
      // bool = 1, char = 1, byte = 1
      // int = 4, uint = 4, short = 2, ushort = 2,
      short size = 1; // PartType(byte)
      size += 8; // int = 4 Parent + Child
      //int length = 0;
      if (Text == null) Text = "";
      size += (short)(Text.Length + 1); // string = 2 + 1
      if (Description == null) Description = "";
      size += (short)(Description.Length + 2); // string = 2 + 1
      if (ColumnRow == null) ColumnRow = [0, 0];
      size += 2; // byte = 1 * 2
      if (Stroke != null)
        size += 4; // Stroke.ToUint = 6 + 1
      if (BackgroundColor != null)
        size += 4; // BackgroundColor.ToUint = 6 + 1
      if (TextColor != null)
        size += 4; // TextColor.ToUint = 6 + 1
      size += 2; // IsEnable = 1, IsVisible = 1
      return size;
    }

    public object Clone()
    {
      return new MapPartModel
      {
        PartType = this.PartType,
        Parent = this.Parent,
        Child = this.Child,
        Text = this.Text,
        Description = this.Description,
        ColumnRow = this.ColumnRow,
        Stroke = this.Stroke,
        BackgroundColor = this.BackgroundColor,
        Image = this.Image,
        TextColor = this.TextColor,
        IsEnable = this.IsEnable,
        IsVisible = this.IsVisible
      };
    }

    public byte[] GetData()
    {
      short index = -1;
      byte[] data = new byte[GetSize()];

      data[++index] = (byte)this.PartType;

      byte[] temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Parent);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(Child);
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      // Text
      byte lenText = Text.Length <= byte.MaxValue ? (byte)Text.Length : byte.MaxValue;
      data[++index] = lenText;
      for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
        data[++index] = (byte)Text[i];

      // Description
      short lenDes = Description.Length <= short.MaxValue ? (short)Description.Length : short.MaxValue;
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(lenDes);
      data[++index] = temp[0];
      data[++index] = temp[1];
      for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
        data[++index] = (byte)Description[i];

      // ColumnRow
      data[++index] = ColumnRow[0];
      data[++index] = ColumnRow[1];

      // Stroke
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(((SolidColorBrush)Stroke).Color.ToUint());
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      // BackgroundColor
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(BackgroundColor.ToUint());
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      // TextColor
      temp = BitConverter.GetBytes(TextColor.ToUint());
      for (int i = 0; i < temp.Length; i++)
        data[++index] = temp[i];

      data[++index] = (byte)BitConverter.GetBytes(IsEnable)[0];
      data[++index] = (byte)BitConverter.GetBytes(IsVisible)[0];

      return data;
    }

    public int LoadData(byte[] data, int index)
    {
      byte[] temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Parent = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);

      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) // int
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Child = BitConverter.ToInt32(temp, 0);

      byte lenText = data[++index];// byte
      for (byte i = 0; i < lenText; i++)
        Text += (char)data[++index];

      temp = new byte[2]; // short
      for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      short lenDes = BitConverter.ToInt16(temp, 0);
      for (short i = 0; i < lenDes; i++)
        Description += (char)data[++index];

      ColumnRow = new[]
        {
            data[++index],
            data[++index]
        };

      temp = new byte[4]; // int
      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      Stroke = new SolidColorBrush(Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0)));

      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      BackgroundColor = Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0));

      for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)
        temp[i] = data[++index];
      TextColor = Color.FromUint(BitConverter.ToUInt32(temp, 0));

      temp = [data[++index]]; // byte
      IsEnable = BitConverter.ToBoolean(temp);

      temp = [data[++index]]; // byte
      IsVisible = BitConverter.ToBoolean(temp);
      return index;
    }
    public void LoadMapPart(IMapPart mapPart)
    {
      mapPart.Parent = Parent;
      mapPart.Child = Child;
      mapPart.Text = Text;
      mapPart.Description = Description;
      mapPart.ColumnRow = ColumnRow;
      mapPart.Stroke = Stroke;
      mapPart.BackgroundColor = BackgroundColor;
      mapPart.Image = Image;
      mapPart.TextColor = TextColor;
      mapPart.IsEnable = IsEnable;
      mapPart.IsVisible = IsVisible;
    }
  }

    public List<byte[]> ColumnRows { get; set; }
    public Brush? Stroke { get; set; }
    public Color? BackgroundColor { get; set; }
    public Color? TextColor { get; set; }
  }

                {

                    {
                        sw.Write(plainText);
                    }
                }
                return Convert.ToBase64String(ms.ToArray());
            }
        }
    }

    public static string Decrypt(string cipherText, string key)
    {
        using (Aes aes = Aes.Create())
        {
            aes.Key = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(key);
            aes.IV = new byte[16]; // Initialization vector with zeros

            ICryptoTransform decryptor = aes.CreateDecryptor(aes.Key, aes.IV);

            using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(Convert.FromBase64String(cipherText)))
            {
                using (CryptoStream cs = new CryptoStream(ms, decryptor, CryptoStreamMode.Read))
                {
                    using (StreamReader sr = new StreamReader(cs))
                    {
                        return sr.ReadToEnd();
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static void Main()
    {
        string key = "thisisaverysecurekey123456"; // Key must be 16, 24, or 32 bytes long
        string original = "Hello, World!";

        string encrypted = Encrypt(original, key);
        Console.WriteLine($"Encrypted: {encrypted}");

        string decrypted = Decrypt(encrypted, key);
        Console.WriteLine($"Decrypted: {decrypted}");
    }

using Microsoft.Maui;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Pages;

public class SplashPage : ContentPage, IDrawable
{
	GraphicsView graphicsView;

	public SplashPage()
	{
		graphicsView = new GraphicsView() { Drawable = this, BackgroundColor = Colors.DarkGreen };
    Content = graphicsView;

    this.Appearing += SplashPage_Appearing;
    this.Disappearing += SplashPage_Disappearing;
	}

  private void SplashPage_Appearing(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
		running = true;
    sw.Start();
    Dispatcher.StartTimer(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(1f / 60), DrawLoop);
  }
  private void SplashPage_Disappearing(object? sender, EventArgs e)
  {
		running = false;
  }

  Stopwatch sw = new Stopwatch();
  int frameCount = 0;
  double fps = 0.0,count;
  const int sampleSize = 60; // Number of frames to sample for FPS calculation
  bool running = false;
  double tick;

  private bool DrawLoop()
  {
    graphicsView.Invalidate();
    frameCount++;
    return running;
	}
  public void Draw(ICanvas canvas, RectF dirtyRect)
  {
		// Clear Screen
		canvas.FillColor = Colors.CornflowerBlue;
		canvas.FillRectangle(dirtyRect);

		canvas.FontColor = Colors.Black;
    canvas.FontSize = 5;
		canvas.DrawString($"FPS : {fps}\n{frameCount}", 10, 10, dirtyRect.Width, dirtyRect.Height, HorizontalAlignment.Left, VerticalAlignment.Top, TextFlow.ClipBounds, 5);

    
    tick = sw.Elapsed.TotalSeconds;
    //count += tick;

    if (tick >= 1)
    {
      // Reset for next sample
      
      frameCount = 0;
      //sw.Reset();
      sw.Restart();
    }
    fps = frameCount / tick;
  }
}

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Pages;

public partial class MainMenu : ContentPage
{
  public MainMenu()
  {
    InitializeComponent();
  }

  private void ContentPage_Appearing(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    _uiMap.SetStats(_uiStats);
    _uiMap.SetProperties(_uiProperties);
    //_uiBehaviours.SetUIWorldMap(_uiMap);
  }
  private void BtnUpActions_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    int index = _stlLeftBar.Children.IndexOf((IView)sender);
    switch (index)
    {
      case 0: // Leader Board
        break;
      case 1: // Store
        break;
      case 2: // Inventory
        break;
      case 3: // Map
        _uiStats.IsEnabled = _uiStats.IsVisible = true;
        _uiProperties.IsEnabled = _uiProperties.IsVisible = false;
        break;
      case 4: // Design
        _uiStats.IsEnabled = _uiStats.IsVisible = false;
        _uiProperties.IsEnabled = _uiProperties.IsVisible = true;
        break;
    }
  }

  private void BtnBottomActions_Clicked(object sender, EventArgs e)
  {
    int index = _stlLeftBar.Children.IndexOf((IView)sender);
    switch (index)
    {
      case 0: // Profile
        break;
      case 1: // Settings
        break;
      case 2: // Action 1
        break;
      case 3: // Action 2
        break;
      case 4: // Action 3
        break;
      case 5: // Action 4
        break;
    }
  }
}

internal static class WindowExtensions
{
  #region Private static fields

  private static Action<nint> _action;
  private static uint _msgId;

  #endregion

  private static readonly string AppName = "Client";
  private static readonly string ClassName = "MauiProgram";
  public static nint hWnd2;
  private static nint hWnd;

  #region Public methods

  public static void RegisterWindowMessage(string lpString, Action<nint> action)
  {
    _msgId = Win32.RegisterWindowMessage(lpString);
    RegisterWindowMessage(_msgId, action);
  }
  public static void RegisterWindowMessage(uint msgId, Action<nint> action)
  {
    _action = action;
    _msgId = msgId;

    WNDCLASSEX wcex = new()
    {
      style = ClassStyles.DoubleClicks
    };
    wcex.cbSize = (uint)Marshal.SizeOf(wcex);
    wcex.lpfnWndProc = WndProc;
    wcex.cbClsExtra = 0;
    wcex.cbWndExtra = 0;
    //wcex.hIcon = Win32.LoadIcon(IntPtr.Zero, (IntPtr)Win32.IDI_APPLICATION);
    //wcex.hCursor = Win32.LoadCursor(IntPtr.Zero, (int)Win32.IDC_ARROW);
    wcex.hIconSm = nint.Zero;
    //wcex.hbrBackground = (IntPtr)(Win32.COLOR_WINDOW + 1);
    wcex.lpszMenuName = null;
    wcex.lpszClassName = ClassName;

    if (RegisterClassEx(ref wcex) == 0)
    {
      //_ = MessageBox(nint.Zero, "RegisterClassEx failed", AppName, (int)(MB_OK | MB_ICONEXCLAMATION | MB_SETFOREGROUND));
      return;
    }

    //uint visible = Win32.WS_OVERLAPPEDWINDOW | Win32.WS_VISIBLE;
    uint invisible = 0;

    var x = 0; // 250;
    var y = 0; // 250;
    var w = 0; // 250;
    var h = 0; // 250;
    hWnd = CreateWindowEx(0, ClassName, AppName, invisible,
        x, y, w, h, nint.Zero, nint.Zero, nint.Zero, nint.Zero);

    if (hWnd == nint.Zero)
    {
      //_ = MessageBox(nint.Zero, "CreateWindow failed", AppName, (int)(MB_OK | MB_ICONEXCLAMATION | MB_SETFOREGROUND));
      return;
    }

    // Main message loop:
    /*int rv;
    while ((rv = GetMessage(out MSG Msg, IntPtr.Zero, 0, 0)) > 0)
    {
        _ = Win32.TranslateMessage(ref Msg);
        _ = Win32.DispatchMessage(ref Msg);
    }*/
  }

  private static nint WndProc(nint hWnd, uint message, nint wParam, nint lParam)
  {
    if (message == _msgId)
    {
      //_action?.Invoke(lParam);
      SetForegroundWindow(hWnd2);
    }
    switch (message)
    {
      case WM_DESTROY:
        PostQuitMessage(0);
        return nint.Zero;
      default:
        return DefWindowProc(hWnd, message, wParam, lParam);
    }
  }
  #endregion
}
#endif
