using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Utilities
{
  public sealed class ConfigFile
  {
    public static ConfigFile? Instance { get; private set; }
    public static void Initialize()
    {
      Instance = new ConfigFile();
    }

    private FileInfo FSI;
    private FileStream fs;

    private ConfigFileModel model;

    private string PGP;

    private ConfigFile()
    {
      if (Instance == null || Instance != this) Instance = this;

      string PGK = ".config";
      PGP = Environment.ProcessPath;
      PGP = PGP.Substring(0, PGP.LastIndexOf('\\'));
      PGP = PGP + "\\" + PGK;

      FSI = new System.IO.FileInfo(PGP);

      fs = FSI.Open(FileMode.OpenOrCreate);
      FSI.Attributes = FileAttributes.Hidden;

      try
      {
        if (fs.Length > 0) model = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ConfigFileModel>(fs);
        else model = new ConfigFileModel();
      }
      catch (Exception ex)
      {
        byte[] array = new byte[FSI.Length];
        fs.Close();
        fs = FSI.Open(FileMode.OpenOrCreate);
        FSI.Attributes = FileAttributes.Hidden;
        fs.Read(array);
        PGK = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(array);
        int index = PGK.IndexOf("}");
        if(index != -1)
        {
          PGK = PGK.Substring(0, index + 1);
        }
        else
        {
          PGK = PGK.Substring(0, index - 1);
          model = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ConfigFileModel>(PGK);
          JsonSerializer.Serialize(fs, model);
        }

        fs.SetLength(0);
        fs.Close();
        fs = FSI.Open(FileMode.OpenOrCreate);
        FSI.Attributes = FileAttributes.Hidden;
        if (fs.Length > 0) model = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ConfigFileModel>(fs);
        else model = new ConfigFileModel();
        //model = new ConfigFileModel();
      }

      //if (FSI.Length <= 0)
      //{
      //  byte[] array = Encoding.ASCII.GetBytes(PG);
      //  fs.Write(array);
      //  fs.Close();
      //}
      //else
      //{
      //  byte[] array = new byte[FSI.Length];
      //  fs.Read(array);
      //  PG = Encoding.ASCII.GetString(array);
      //  fs.Close();
      //}

      fs.Close();
    }

    public string GetGuid()
    {
      string guid;

      if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(model.GuidValue))
      {
        guid = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
        model.GuidValue = guid.ToString();

        fs = FSI.Open(FileMode.OpenOrCreate);
        FSI.Attributes = FileAttributes.Hidden;

        JsonSerializer.Serialize(fs, model);
      }
      else
      {
        guid = model.GuidValue;
      }

      fs.Close();
      return guid;
    }

#if WINDOWS
    public IntPtr GetWindowHandler()
    {
      IntPtr hWnd;

      fs = FSI.Open(FileMode.OpenOrCreate);
      FSI.Attributes = FileAttributes.Hidden;

      model = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<ConfigFileModel>(fs);
      hWnd = model.WindowHandle;

      fs.Close();
      return hWnd;
    }

    public void SetWindowHandle(nint hWnd)
    {
      model.WindowHandle = (int)hWnd;

      fs = FSI.Open(FileMode.OpenOrCreate);
      FSI.Attributes = FileAttributes.Hidden;

      JsonSerializer.Serialize(fs, model);
      fs.Close();
    }
#endif
  }
}

#if WINDOWS
using Microsoft.UI.Input;
using Windows.Gaming.Input;
using Windows.System;
using Windows.UI.Core;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Utilities
{
  public enum DevicesState
  {
    Keyboard, Gamepad, ArcadeStick, FlightStick
  }
  public class GameKeyBoard
  {
    public static GameKeyBoard Instance { get; private set; }
    public static void Initialise()
    {
      Instance = new GameKeyBoard();
    }


    private bool keyDown;
    public bool KeyDown
    {
      get
      {
        if (keyDown)
        {
          keyDown = false;
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      }
      set { keyDown = value; }
    }
    public Dictionary<int, bool> KeysValues { get; private set; } = new();
    private Dictionary<int, bool> keysUp { get; set; } = new();

    private readonly List<VirtualKey> virtualKeys = new();
    private List<CoreVirtualKeyStates> keysStates = new();
    private List<DevicesState> Devices { get; set; } = new();

    private GameKeyBoard()
    {
      ArcadeStick.ArcadeStickAdded += ArcadeStick_ArcadeStickAdded;
      ArcadeStick.ArcadeStickRemoved += ArcadeStick_ArcadeStickRemoved;
      FlightStick.FlightStickAdded += FlightStick_FlightStickAdded;
      FlightStick.FlightStickRemoved += FlightStick_FlightStickRemoved;
      Gamepad.GamepadAdded += Gamepad_GamepadAdded;
      Gamepad.GamepadRemoved += Gamepad_GamepadRemoved;

      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.Left);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.Right);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.Up);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.Down);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.Enter);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.Control);

      //Type type = typeof(VirtualKey);
      //IEnumerable<VirtualKey> VirtualKeys = type.GetEnumValues().Cast<VirtualKey>();

      //virtualKeys.AddRange(VirtualKeys);

      for (int i = 0; i < virtualKeys.Count; i++)
      {
        KeysValues.Add(i, false);
        keysUp.Add(i, true);
        keysStates.Add(CoreVirtualKeyStates.None);
      }
      Devices.Add(DevicesState.Keyboard);
    }

    #region Events
    private void Gamepad_GamepadRemoved(object sender, Gamepad e)
    {
      Devices.Remove(DevicesState.Gamepad);
      virtualKeys.Remove(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadLeft);
      virtualKeys.Remove(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadRight);
      virtualKeys.Remove(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadUp);
      virtualKeys.Remove(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadDown);
      for (int i = virtualKeys.Count - 4; i < virtualKeys.Count; i++)
      {
        KeysValues.Remove(i);
        keysUp.Remove(i);
        keysStates.RemoveAt(i);
      }
    }
    private void Gamepad_GamepadAdded(object sender, Gamepad e)
    {
      for (int i = virtualKeys.Count; i < virtualKeys.Count + 4; i++)
      {
        KeysValues.Add(i, false);
        keysUp.Add(i, true);
        keysStates.Insert(i, CoreVirtualKeyStates.None);
      }
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadLeft);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadRight);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadUp);
      virtualKeys.Add(VirtualKey.GamepadDPadDown);
      Devices.Add(DevicesState.Gamepad);
    }

    private void FlightStick_FlightStickRemoved(object sender, FlightStick e)
    {
      //Devices.Remove(DevicesState.FlightStick);
    }
    private void FlightStick_FlightStickAdded(object sender, FlightStick e)
    {
      //Devices.Add(DevicesState.FlightStick);
    }

    private void ArcadeStick_ArcadeStickRemoved(object sender, ArcadeStick e)
    {
      //Devices.Remove(DevicesState.ArcadeStick);
    }
    private void ArcadeStick_ArcadeStickAdded(object sender, Windows.Gaming.Input.ArcadeStick e)
    {
      //Devices.Add(DevicesState.ArcadeStick);
    }
    #endregion

    public void Update()
    {
      int i;
      for (i = 0; i < virtualKeys.Count - 1; i++)
      {
        keysStates[i] = InputKeyboardSource.GetKeyStateForCurrentThread(virtualKeys[i]);
        switch (keysStates[i])
        {
          case CoreVirtualKeyStates.None:
            keysUp[i] = true;
            KeysValues[i] = false;
            break;
          case CoreVirtualKeyStates.Down:
            if (keysUp[i])
            {
              keysUp[i] = false;
              KeysValues[i] = true;
            }
            KeyDown = true;
            break;
          case CoreVirtualKeyStates.Down | CoreVirtualKeyStates.Locked:
            if (keysUp[i])
            {
              keysUp[i] = false;
              KeysValues[i] = true;
            }
            KeyDown = true;
            break;
          case CoreVirtualKeyStates.Locked:
            keysUp[i] = true;
            KeysValues[i] = false;
            break;
        }
      }

      // Check Controll Key
      keysStates[i] = InputKeyboardSource.GetKeyStateForCurrentThread(virtualKeys[i]);
      switch (keysStates[i])
      {
        case CoreVirtualKeyStates.None:
          KeysValues[i] = false;
          break;
        case CoreVirtualKeyStates.Down:
          KeysValues[i] = true;
          break;
        case CoreVirtualKeyStates.Down | CoreVirtualKeyStates.Locked:
          KeysValues[i] = true;
          break;
        case CoreVirtualKeyStates.Locked:
          KeysValues[i] = false;
          break;
      }
    }
  }
}
#endif

  internal static class Helper
  {
    public static Random rnd { get; set; } = new();

    public static void Initialise()
    {
      Activities.Initialize();
      BattleSystem.Initialize();
      Profile.Initialize();
#if WINDOWS
      //GameKeyBoard.Initialise();
#endif

      //ScreenManager.Initialise();
      //MainResources.Initialize();
    }

    internal static void StartTimer()
    {
      //GamePage.StartTimer();
    }

    internal static void PauseTimer()
    {
      //GamePage.PauseTimer();
    }
    internal static void StopTimer()
    {
      //GamePage.StopTimer();
    }

    public static void ConvertToRgb(float hue, float saturation, float luminosity, out float r, out float g, out float b)
    {
      if (luminosity == 0)
      {
        r = g = b = 0;
        return;
      }

      if (saturation == 0)
      {
        r = g = b = luminosity;
        return;
      }
      float temp2 = luminosity <= 0.5f ? luminosity * (1.0f + saturation) : luminosity + saturation - luminosity * saturation;
      float temp1 = 2.0f * luminosity - temp2;

      var t3 = new[] { hue + 1.0f / 3.0f, hue, hue - 1.0f / 3.0f };
      var clr = new float[] { 0, 0, 0 };
      for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++)
      {
        if (t3[i] < 0)
          t3[i] += 1.0f;
        if (t3[i] > 1)
          t3[i] -= 1.0f;
        if (6.0 * t3[i] < 1.0)
          clr[i] = temp1 + (temp2 - temp1) * t3[i] * 6.0f;
        else if (2.0 * t3[i] < 1.0)
          clr[i] = temp2;
        else if (3.0 * t3[i] < 2.0)
          clr[i] = temp1 + (temp2 - temp1) * (2.0f / 3.0f - t3[i]) * 6.0f;
        else
          clr[i] = temp1;
      }

      r = clr[0];
      g = clr[1];
      b = clr[2];
    }
  }
}

using System;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Utilities
{
  public class MainResources
  {

    private const string BASE_PATH = "EmojiWorld.Resources.";

    public static MainResources Instance { get; private set; }
    public static void Initialize()
    {
      if (Instance == null)
        Instance = new MainResources();

    }

    private bool _isReady;
    public bool IsReady
    {
      get
      {
        if (_isReady)
        {
          _isReady = false;
          return true;
        }
        return false;
      }
      private set
      {
        _isReady = value;
      }
    }
    //public readonly Dictionary<string, SKImage> SKImages = new();

    private MainResources()
    {
      IsReady = false;
      //LoadImages("Lights", "png", "lightmask");
      IsReady = true;
    }

    public ImageSource LoadImage(string path)
    {
      if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(path)) return null;
      string _path = BASE_PATH + "Images";
      Assembly assembly = GetType().GetTypeInfo().Assembly;
      using (Stream stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream($"{_path}.{path}"))
      {
        byte[] bytes = new byte[stream.Length];
        stream.Read(bytes, 0, bytes.Length);
        MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream(bytes);
        return ImageSource.FromStream(() => memoryStream);
      }
      return null;
    }
    //public void LoadImages(string subpath, string extention, params string[] paths)
    //{
    //  if (paths == null || paths.Length == 0) return;
    //  string path = BASE_PATH + "Images";
    //  SKImage image;
    //  Assembly assembly = GetType().GetTypeInfo().Assembly;
    //  for (int i = 0; i < paths.Length; i++)
    //  {
    //    //name = paths[i][..paths[i].LastIndexOf('.')];
    //    if (SKImages.ContainsKey(paths[i])) continue;
    //    using (Stream stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream($"{path}.{subpath}.{paths[i]}.{extention}"))
    //    {
    //      image = SKImage.FromEncodedData(stream);
    //      SKImages.Add(paths[i], image);
    //    }

    //  }
    //}

    public string LoadLevel(string level)
    {
      string path = BASE_PATH + "Raw." + level;
      Assembly assembly = GetType().GetTypeInfo().Assembly;
      using (Stream stream = assembly.GetManifestResourceStream(path))
      {
        using var reader = new StreamReader(stream);
        path = reader.ReadToEnd();
      }
      return path;
    }
  }
}

using ReGame.SharedCode.Utilities;
using Microsoft.Maui.Handlers;
using System.Diagnostics;


#if WINDOWS
using Microsoft.UI;
using Microsoft.UI.Windowing;
using Windows.Graphics;
#endif

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Utilities
{
  public sealed class Settings
  {
    public static Settings? Instance { get; private set; }
    public static void Initialize()
    {
      Instance = new Settings();
    }

    private Settings()
    {
    }

#if WINDOWS // TOTO: Edit IMAGESLOADED

    private const byte X = 0, Y = 1, WIDTH = 2, HEIGHT = 3, STATE = 4;

    private string[] propertiesNames;
    private int[] propertiesValues;
    private int count;
    public void InitWindow(IWindowHandler handler, IDispatcher dispatcher)
    {
      if (handler == null) return;

      propertiesNames = new string[] { "appWindow.Position.X", "appWindow.Position.Y", "appWindow.Size.Width", "appWindow.Size.Height", "appWindow.OverlappedPresenterState" };
      propertiesValues = new int[propertiesNames.Length];

      var mauiWindow = handler.VirtualView;
      var nativeWindow = handler.PlatformView;
      IntPtr windowHandle = WinRT.Interop.WindowNative.GetWindowHandle(nativeWindow);

      ConfigFile.Instance?.SetWindowHandle(windowHandle);

      //SharedCode.Utility.WindowExtensions.hWnd2 = windowHandle;
      //SharedCode.Utility.WindowExtensions.RegisterWindowMessage(Win32.WM_SHOWME, null);

      WindowId windowId = Microsoft.UI.Win32Interop.GetWindowIdFromWindow(windowHandle);
      AppWindow appWindow = Microsoft.UI.Windowing.AppWindow.GetFromWindowId(windowId);

      int width = 0, height = 0;
      int posX = 0, posY = 0;

      if (Preferences.Default.ContainsKey(propertiesNames[WIDTH]))
        width = Preferences.Default.Get<int>(propertiesNames[WIDTH], width);
      else
      {
        width = appWindow.Size.Width;
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[WIDTH], appWindow.Size.Width);
      }
      if (Preferences.Default.ContainsKey(propertiesNames[HEIGHT]))
        height = Preferences.Default.Get<int>(propertiesNames[HEIGHT], height);
      else
      {
        height = appWindow.Size.Height;
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[HEIGHT], appWindow.Size.Height);
      }
      appWindow.Resize(new SizeInt32(width, height));
      propertiesValues[WIDTH] = width; propertiesValues[HEIGHT] = height;

      if (Preferences.Default.ContainsKey(propertiesNames[X]))
        posX = Preferences.Default.Get<int>(propertiesNames[X], posX);
      else
      {
        posX = appWindow.Position.X;
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[X], appWindow.Position.X);
      }
      if (Preferences.Default.ContainsKey(propertiesNames[Y]))
        posY = Preferences.Default.Get<int>(propertiesNames[Y], posY);
      else
      {
        posY = appWindow.Position.Y;
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[Y], appWindow.Position.Y);
      }
      appWindow.Move(new PointInt32(posX, posY));
      propertiesValues[X] = posX; propertiesValues[Y] = posY;

      OverlappedPresenterState state = ((OverlappedPresenter)appWindow.Presenter).State;
      if (Preferences.Default.ContainsKey(propertiesNames[STATE]))
      {
        state = (OverlappedPresenterState)Preferences.Default.Get<int>(propertiesNames[STATE], (int)state);
        if (state == OverlappedPresenterState.Maximized)
        {
          ((OverlappedPresenter)appWindow.Presenter).Maximize();
        }
      }
      else
      {
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[STATE], (int)state);
      }
      propertiesValues[STATE] = (int)state;

      Application.Current.Windows[0].PropertyChanged += (sender, e) =>
      {
        dispatcher.DispatchDelayed(TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(100), () =>
        {
          if (appWindow.Presenter != null)
            state = ((OverlappedPresenter)appWindow.Presenter).State;
          if (state != (OverlappedPresenterState)propertiesValues[STATE])
          {
            switch (state)
            {
              case OverlappedPresenterState.Maximized:
              case OverlappedPresenterState.Restored:
                propertiesValues[STATE] = (int)state;
                break;
              case OverlappedPresenterState.Minimized:
                break;
            }
            Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[STATE], propertiesValues[STATE]);
          }
          else if (state == OverlappedPresenterState.Restored)
          {
            propertiesValues[X] = appWindow.Position.X; propertiesValues[Y] = appWindow.Position.Y;
            propertiesValues[WIDTH] = appWindow.Size.Width;
            propertiesValues[HEIGHT] = appWindow.Size.Height;
          }
        });
      };

      nativeWindow.Activate();
      //nativeWindow.Activated += (sender, e) =>
      //{
      //  Preferences.Default.Set<bool>(propertiesNames[IMAGESLOADED], Shell.Current.FlyoutIsPresented);
      //};
      nativeWindow.Closed += (sender, e) =>
      {
        Helper.StopTimer();

        state = ((OverlappedPresenter)appWindow.Presenter).State;

        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[WIDTH], propertiesValues[WIDTH]);
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[HEIGHT], propertiesValues[HEIGHT]);
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[X], propertiesValues[X]);
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[Y], propertiesValues[Y]);
        Preferences.Default.Set<int>(propertiesNames[STATE], propertiesValues[STATE]);
      };
    }
#endif
  }
}

#if WINDOWS
using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Input;
using Microsoft.UI.Xaml;
using Microsoft.UI.Input;
#elif ANDROID
using Android.Views;
#endif
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls;
using Microsoft.Maui.Controls.Platform;
using Microsoft.Maui.Platform;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.Utilities
{
  internal delegate void TouchActionEventHandler(object sender, TouchActionEventArgs e);
  internal enum TouchActionType
  {
    Entered,
    Pressed,
    Moved,
    Released,
    Exited,
    Cancelled
  }
  internal class TouchActionEventArgs : EventArgs
  {
    public TouchActionEventArgs(long id, TouchActionType type, Point location, bool isInContact)
    {
      Id = id;
      Type = type;
      Location = location;
      IsInContact = isInContact;
    }

    public long Id { private set; get; }

    public TouchActionType Type { private set; get; }

    public Point Location { private set; get; }

    public bool IsInContact { private set; get; }
  }

  internal class TouchTracking : RoutingEffect
  {
    public event TouchActionEventHandler TouchAction;

    public bool Capture { set; get; }

    public void OnTouchAction(Element element, TouchActionEventArgs e)
    {
      TouchAction?.Invoke(element, e);
    }
  }

#if ANDROID
  internal class TouchPlatformEffect : PlatformEffect
  {
    Android.Views.View view;
    Element formsElement;
    TouchTracking libTouchEffect;//effect
    bool capture;
    Func<double, double> fromPixels;
    int[] twoIntArray = new int[2];

    static Dictionary<Android.Views.View, TouchPlatformEffect> viewDictionary =
        new Dictionary<Android.Views.View, TouchPlatformEffect>();
    static Dictionary<int, TouchPlatformEffect> idToEffectDictionary =
        new Dictionary<int, TouchPlatformEffect>();

    protected override void OnAttached()
    {
      // Get the Android View corresponding to the Element that the effect is attached to
      view = Control == null ? Container : Control;

      // Get access to the TouchEffect class in the .NET Standard library
      TouchTracking touchEffect = (TouchTracking)Element.Effects.FirstOrDefault(e => e is TouchTracking);

      if (touchEffect != null && view != null)
      {
        viewDictionary.Add(view, this);

        formsElement = Element;

        libTouchEffect = touchEffect;

        // Save fromPixels function
        fromPixels = view.Context.FromPixels;

        // Set event handler on View
        view.Touch += OnTouch;
      }
    }

    protected override void OnDetached()
    {
      if (viewDictionary.ContainsKey(view))
      {
        viewDictionary.Remove(view);
        view.Touch -= OnTouch;
      }
    }

    void OnTouch(object sender, Android.Views.View.TouchEventArgs args)
    {
      // Two object common to all the events
      Android.Views.View senderView = sender as Android.Views.View;
      MotionEvent motionEvent = args.Event;

      // Get the pointer index
      int pointerIndex = motionEvent.ActionIndex;

      // Get the id that identifies a finger over the course of its progress
      int id = motionEvent.GetPointerId(pointerIndex);

      senderView.GetLocationOnScreen(twoIntArray);
      Point screenPointerCoords = new Point(twoIntArray[0] + motionEvent.GetX(pointerIndex), twoIntArray[1] + motionEvent.GetY(pointerIndex));


      // Use ActionMasked here rather than Action to reduce the number of possibilities
      switch (args.Event.ActionMasked)
      {
        case MotionEventActions.Down:
        case MotionEventActions.PointerDown:
          FireEvent(this, id, TouchActionType.Pressed, screenPointerCoords, true);

          idToEffectDictionary.Add(id, this);

          capture = libTouchEffect.Capture;
          break;

        case MotionEventActions.Move:
          // Multiple Move events are bundled, so handle them in a loop
          for (pointerIndex = 0; pointerIndex < motionEvent.PointerCount; pointerIndex++)
          {
            id = motionEvent.GetPointerId(pointerIndex);

            if (capture)
            {
              senderView.GetLocationOnScreen(twoIntArray);
              
              screenPointerCoords = new Point(twoIntArray[0] + motionEvent.GetX(pointerIndex), twoIntArray[1] + motionEvent.GetY(pointerIndex));

              FireEvent(this, id, TouchActionType.Moved, screenPointerCoords, true);
            }
            else
            {
              CheckForBoundaryHop(id, screenPointerCoords);

              if (idToEffectDictionary[id] != null)
              {
                FireEvent(idToEffectDictionary[id], id, TouchActionType.Moved, screenPointerCoords, true);
              }
            }
          }
          break;

        case MotionEventActions.Up:
        case MotionEventActions.Pointer1Up:
          if (capture)
          {
            FireEvent(this, id, TouchActionType.Released, screenPointerCoords, false);
          }
          else
          {
            CheckForBoundaryHop(id, screenPointerCoords);

            if (idToEffectDictionary[id] != null)
            {
              FireEvent(idToEffectDictionary[id], id, TouchActionType.Released, screenPointerCoords, false);
            }
          }
          idToEffectDictionary.Remove(id);
          break;

        case MotionEventActions.Cancel:
          if (capture)
          {
            FireEvent(this, id, TouchActionType.Cancelled, screenPointerCoords, false);
          }
          else
          {
            if (idToEffectDictionary[id] != null)
            {
              FireEvent(idToEffectDictionary[id], id, TouchActionType.Cancelled, screenPointerCoords, false);
            }
          }
          idToEffectDictionary.Remove(id);
          break;
      }
    }

    void CheckForBoundaryHop(int id, Point pointerLocation)
    {
      TouchPlatformEffect touchEffectHit = null;

      foreach (Android.Views.View view in viewDictionary.Keys)
      {
        // Get the view rectangle
        try
        {
          view.GetLocationOnScreen(twoIntArray);
        }
        catch // System.ObjectDisposedException: Cannot access a disposed object.
        {
          continue;
        }
        Rect viewRect = new Rect(twoIntArray[0], twoIntArray[1], view.Width, view.Height);

        if (viewRect.Contains(pointerLocation))
        {
          touchEffectHit = viewDictionary[view];
        }
      }

      if (touchEffectHit != idToEffectDictionary[id])
      {
        if (idToEffectDictionary[id] != null)
        {
          FireEvent(idToEffectDictionary[id], id, TouchActionType.Exited, pointerLocation, true);
        }
        if (touchEffectHit != null)
        {
          FireEvent(touchEffectHit, id, TouchActionType.Entered, pointerLocation, true);
        }
        idToEffectDictionary[id] = touchEffectHit;
      }
    }

    void FireEvent(TouchPlatformEffect touchEffect, int id, TouchActionType actionType, Point pointerLocation, bool isInContact)
    {
      // Get the method to call for firing events
      Action<Element, TouchActionEventArgs> onTouchAction = touchEffect.libTouchEffect.OnTouchAction;

      // Get the location of the pointer within the view
      touchEffect.view.GetLocationOnScreen(twoIntArray);
      float x = (float)pointerLocation.X - twoIntArray[0];
      float y = (float)pointerLocation.Y - twoIntArray[1];
      Point point = new Point(x, y);
      //SKPoint point = new SKPoint((float)fromPixels(x), (float)fromPixels(y));

      // Call the method
      onTouchAction(touchEffect.formsElement,
          new TouchActionEventArgs(id, actionType, point, isInContact));
    }
  }
#elif IOS
internal class TouchPlatformEffect : PlatformEffect
{
    protected override void OnAttached()
    {
        // Customize the control here
    }

    protected override void OnDetached()
    {
        // Cleanup the control customization here
    }
}
#elif WINDOWS
  internal class TouchPlatformEffect : PlatformEffect
  {
    FrameworkElement frameworkElement;
    TouchTracking effect;
    Action<Element, TouchActionEventArgs> onTouchAction;

    protected override void OnAttached()
    {
      // Get the Windows FrameworkElement corresponding to the Element that the effect is attached to
      frameworkElement = Control == null ? Container : Control;

      // Get access to the TouchEffect class in the .NET Standard library
      effect = (TouchTracking)Element.Effects.FirstOrDefault(e => e is RoutingEffect);

      if (effect != null && frameworkElement != null)
      {
        // Save the method to call on touch events
        onTouchAction = effect.OnTouchAction;

        // Set event handlers on FrameworkElement
        frameworkElement.PointerEntered += OnPointerEntered;
        frameworkElement.PointerPressed += OnPointerPressed;
        frameworkElement.PointerMoved += OnPointerMoved;
        frameworkElement.PointerReleased += OnPointerReleased;
        frameworkElement.PointerExited += OnPointerExited;
        frameworkElement.PointerCanceled += OnPointerCancelled;
      }
    }

    protected override void OnDetached()
    {
      if (onTouchAction != null)
      {
        // Release event handlers on FrameworkElement
        frameworkElement.PointerEntered -= OnPointerEntered;
        frameworkElement.PointerPressed -= OnPointerPressed;
        frameworkElement.PointerMoved -= OnPointerMoved;
        frameworkElement.PointerReleased -= OnPointerReleased;
        frameworkElement.PointerExited -= OnPointerEntered;
        frameworkElement.PointerCanceled -= OnPointerCancelled;
      }
    }

    void OnPointerEntered(object sender, PointerRoutedEventArgs args)
    {
      CommonHandler(sender, TouchActionType.Entered, args);
    }

    void OnPointerPressed(object sender, PointerRoutedEventArgs args)
    {
      CommonHandler(sender, TouchActionType.Pressed, args);

      // Check setting of Capture property
      if (effect.Capture)
      {
        (sender as FrameworkElement).CapturePointer(args.Pointer);
      }
    }

    void OnPointerMoved(object sender, PointerRoutedEventArgs args)
    {
      CommonHandler(sender, TouchActionType.Moved, args);
    }

    void OnPointerReleased(object sender, PointerRoutedEventArgs args)
    {
      CommonHandler(sender, TouchActionType.Released, args);
    }

    void OnPointerExited(object sender, PointerRoutedEventArgs args)
    {
      CommonHandler(sender, TouchActionType.Exited, args);
    }

    void OnPointerCancelled(object sender, PointerRoutedEventArgs args)
    {
      CommonHandler(sender, TouchActionType.Cancelled, args);
    }

    void CommonHandler(object sender, TouchActionType touchActionType, PointerRoutedEventArgs args)
    {
      PointerPoint pointerPoint = args.GetCurrentPoint(sender as UIElement);
      Windows.Foundation.Point windowsPoint = pointerPoint.Position;

      onTouchAction(Element, new TouchActionEventArgs(args.Pointer.PointerId, touchActionType, new Point((float)windowsPoint.X, (float)windowsPoint.Y), args.Pointer.IsInContact));
    }
  }
#endif
}

using ReGame.SharedCode.Models;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.ComponentModel;
using System.Runtime.CompilerServices;

namespace ReGame.SharedCode.ViewModels
{
  public class VMWorldMap : INotifyPropertyChanged
  {
    public ObservableCollection<MapPartModel> MapParts { get; set; } = new();
    public ObservableCollection<string> Navigations { get; set; } = new();
    public ObservableCollection<int> Locations { get; set; } = new();
    
    public const int Columns = 11, Rows = 5;

    public event PropertyChangedEventHandler? PropertyChanged;

    public VMWorldMap()
    {
      Init_Collections();

    }

    public void Init_Collections()
    {
      for (int i = 0; i < 50; i++)
      {
        Navigations.Add("AAAAAAA");
      }
      for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++)
      {
        Locations.Add(i);
      }
      for (int j = 0; j < Rows; j++)
      {
        for (int i = 0; i < Columns; i++)
        {
          MapParts.Add(new MapPartModel() { Text = "World"+(i + j + 1) * (j + 1), Description = "Description", BackgroundColor = Color.FromArgb("282828"), Stroke = Color.FromArgb("1E1E1E"), Parent = -1, Child = 0 });

        }
      }
    }

    public void OnPropertyChanged([CallerMemberName] string name = "") =>
        PropertyChanged?.Invoke(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(name));
  }
}

            _show = Win32.SW_MAXIMIZE;

            _show = Win32.SW_MINIMIZE;
            break;
          case OverlappedPresenterState.Restored:
            _show = Win32.SW_RESTORE;
            break;
